        return self.rows(sql + " ORDER BY plate_number", params)

    def customers(self, search: str = "") -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        sql = "SELECT * FROM customers"
        params: list[Any] = []
        if search:
            like = f"%{search}%"
            sql += (
                " WHERE (full_name LIKE ? OR email LIKE ? OR phone LIKE ? OR id_type LIKE ?"
                " OR id_number LIKE ? OR license_number LIKE ?)"
            )
            params.extend([like, like, like, like, like, like])
        return self.rows(sql + " ORDER BY full_name", params)

    def bookings(self, status: str = "", search: str = "") -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        sql = (
            "SELECT b.*, v.plate_number, v.brand, v.model, c.full_name AS customer_name"
            " FROM bookings b JOIN vehicles v ON v.id = b.vehicle_id"
            " JOIN customers c ON c.id = b.customer_id WHERE 1=1"
        )
        params: list[Any] = []
        if status and status != "all":
            sql += " AND b.status = ?"
            params.append(status)
        if search:
            like = f"%{search}%"
            sql += (
                " AND (b.booking_code LIKE ? OR c.full_name LIKE ?"
                " OR v.model LIKE ? OR v.plate_number LIKE ?"
                " OR b.destination LIKE ? OR b.pickup_location LIKE ?)"
            )
            params.extend([like, like, like, like, like, like])
        return self.rows(sql + " ORDER BY b.start_date DESC", params)

    def transactions(self, entry_type: str = "", search: str = "") -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        sql = (
            "SELECT t.*, b.booking_code FROM transactions t"
            " LEFT JOIN bookings b ON b.id = t.booking_id WHERE 1=1"
        )
        params: list[Any] = []
        if entry_type and entry_type != "all":
            sql += " AND t.entry_type = ?"
            params.append(entry_type)
        if search:
            like = f"%{search}%"
            sql += " AND (t.reference LIKE ? OR t.recorded_by LIKE ? OR b.booking_code LIKE ?)"
            params.extend([like, like, like])
        return self.rows(sql + " ORDER BY t.paid_at DESC", params)

    def zones(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        if self._zones_cache is None:
            self._zones_cache = self.rows("SELECT * FROM flood_zones ORDER BY name")
        return self._zones_cache

    def alerts(self, status: str = "") -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        sql = "SELECT * FROM alerts"
        params: list[Any] = []
        if status and status != "all":
            sql += " WHERE status = ?"
            params.append(status)
        return self.rows(sql + " ORDER BY created_at DESC", params)

    def users(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.rows("SELECT * FROM users ORDER BY id")

    def settings(self) -> dict[str, str]:
        if self._settings_cache is None:
            self._settings_cache = {
                row["key"]: row["value"] for row in self.rows("SELECT key, value FROM settings")
            }
        return self._settings_cache

    def setting(self, key: str, default: str = "") -> str:
        """Single-key read with a default — avoids the .get(key, default) chain."""
        return self.settings().get(key, default)

    def set_setting(self, key: str, value: str, actor: str = "system") -> None:
        now = datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT)
        self.execute(
            "INSERT INTO settings (key, value, updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?)"
            " ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value, updated_at = excluded.updated_at",
            (key, value, now),
        )
        if self._settings_cache is not None:
            self._settings_cache[key] = value
        self.audit(actor, "setting_change", "setting", None, f"{key}={value}")

    # -- audit log --------------------------------------------------------
    def audit(
        self,
        username: str,
        action: str,
        entity_type: str = "",
        entity_id: int | None = None,
        details: str = "",
    ) -> None:
        """Record one audit entry. Safe to call from any mutation — never raises."""
        try:
            now = datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT)
            self.execute(
                "INSERT INTO audit_log (username, action, entity_type, entity_id, details, created_at)"
                " VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)",
                (username or "system", action, entity_type, entity_id, details, now),
            )
        except Exception:
            log.exception("audit log write failed")

    def audit_logs(self, limit: int = 200, action: str = "") -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        sql = "SELECT * FROM audit_log"
        params: list[Any] = []
        if action and action != "all":
            sql += " WHERE action = ?"
            params.append(action)
        sql += " ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT ?"
        params.append(int(limit))
        return self.rows(sql, params)

    # -- authentication ---------------------------------------------------
    def login(self, username: str, password: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        username_clean = username.strip()
        account = self.row("SELECT * FROM users WHERE username = ?", (username_clean,))
        if account is None or not account["is_active"]:
            self.audit(
                username_clean or "unknown",
                "login_failed",
                "user",
                None,
                "account not found or disabled",
            )
            raise ValueError("That account does not exist or is disabled.")
        if not verify_password(password, account["password_salt"], account["password_hash"]):
            self.audit(
                username_clean, "login_failed", "user", int(account["id"]), "incorrect password"
            )
            raise ValueError("Incorrect password. Try admin / admin123.")
        self.execute(
            "UPDATE users SET last_seen = ? WHERE id = ?",
            (datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT), account["id"]),
        )
        self.audit(username_clean, "login_success", "user", int(account["id"]))
        return account

    # -- derived ----------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def closest_zone(
        zones: list[dict[str, Any]], lat: float | None, lon: float | None
    ) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], float] | None:
        """Nearest active zone from a pre-fetched list."""
        if lat is None or lon is None:
            return None
        best: tuple[dict[str, Any], float] | None = None
        for zone in zones:
            if not zone["is_active"]:
                continue
            kilometres = distance_km(lat, lon, zone["latitude"], zone["longitude"])
            if best is None or kilometres < best[1]:
                best = (zone, kilometres)
        return best

    def nearest_zone(self, lat: float, lon: float) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], float] | None:
        return self.closest_zone(self.zones(), lat, lon)

    def exposure(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        try:
            buffer = float(self.settings().get("zone_buffer_km", "0.5") or 0)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            buffer = 0.0
        # Fetch zones ONCE — previously this called nearest_zone() per vehicle,
        # and nearest_zone() called zones() each time, producing an N+1 query
        # pattern that touched the database once per unit on every refresh.
        zones = self.zones()
        rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for vehicle in self.vehicles():
            if vehicle["latitude"] is None or vehicle["longitude"] is None:
                continue
            found = self.closest_zone(zones, vehicle["latitude"], vehicle["longitude"])
            if not found:
                continue
            zone, kilometres = found
            rows.append(
                {
                    **vehicle,
                    "zone_name": zone["name"],
                    "zone_risk": zone["risk_level"],
                    "distance_km": kilometres,
                    "inside": kilometres <= zone["radius_km"] + buffer,
                }
            )
        return sorted(rows, key=lambda item: item["distance_km"])

    def dashboard(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        # Single GROUP BY query replaces loading the entire vehicles + bookings
        # tables into Python and iterating them twice each.
        vehicle_counts = {"available": 0, "rented": 0, "maintenance": 0}
        for row in self.rows("SELECT status, COUNT(*) AS n FROM vehicles GROUP BY status"):
            if row["status"] in vehicle_counts:
                vehicle_counts[row["status"]] = int(row["n"])
        booking_stats = self.row(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total,"
            " COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN status = 'ongoing' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) AS ongoing,"
            " COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN status != 'cancelled' THEN total_amount ELSE 0 END), 0) AS value"
            " FROM bookings"
        ) or {"total": 0, "ongoing": 0, "value": 0}
        open_alerts = self.value("SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM alerts WHERE status != 'resolved'")
        return {
            "vehicles": sum(vehicle_counts.values()),
            "available": vehicle_counts["available"],
            "rented": vehicle_counts["rented"],
            "maintenance": vehicle_counts["maintenance"],
            "bookings": int(booking_stats["total"] or 0),
            "ongoing": int(booking_stats["ongoing"] or 0),
            "value": float(booking_stats["value"] or 0),
            "open_alerts": int(open_alerts or 0),
            "utilization": round(
                (
                    (vehicle_counts["rented"] / sum(vehicle_counts.values()) * 100)
                    if sum(vehicle_counts.values()) > 0
                    else 0.0
                ),
                1,
            ),
        }

    def top_vehicles(self, limit: int = 5) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Most-rented vehicles by completed+ongoing booking count."""
        return self.rows(
            "SELECT v.id, v.plate_number, v.brand, v.model, v.category,"
            " COUNT(b.id) AS booking_count,"
            " COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN b.status != 'cancelled' THEN b.total_amount"
            " ELSE 0 END), 0) AS revenue"
            " FROM vehicles v LEFT JOIN bookings b ON b.vehicle_id = v.id"
            " WHERE b.status IN ('ongoing', 'completed')"
            " GROUP BY v.id ORDER BY booking_count DESC LIMIT ?",
            (int(limit),),
        )

    def top_customers(self, limit: int = 5) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Top customers by lifetime spend (non-cancelled bookings)."""
        return self.rows(
            "SELECT c.id, c.full_name, c.phone,"
            " COUNT(b.id) AS booking_count,"
            " COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN b.status != 'cancelled' THEN b.total_amount"
            " ELSE 0 END), 0) AS lifetime_value"
            " FROM customers c LEFT JOIN bookings b ON b.customer_id = c.id"
            " GROUP BY c.id ORDER BY lifetime_value DESC LIMIT ?",
            (int(limit),),
        )

    def monthly_revenue(self, months: int = 6) -> list[tuple[str, float]]:
        return self.collections_by_month()

    def monthly_booking_trends(self, months: int = 6) -> list[tuple[str, int]]:
        rows = self.rows(
            "SELECT substr(start_date, 1, 7) AS month, COUNT(*) AS n"
            " FROM bookings GROUP BY month ORDER BY month"
        )
        return [(row["month"], int(row["n"] or 0)) for row in rows]

    def recent_activity(self, limit: int = 8) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Recent audit-log entries for the dashboard activity feed."""
        return self.audit_logs(limit=limit)

    def collections_by_month(self) -> list[tuple[str, float]]:
        rows = self.rows(
            "SELECT substr(paid_at, 1, 7) AS month,"
            " SUM(CASE WHEN entry_type = 'refund' THEN -amount ELSE amount END) AS total"
            " FROM transactions GROUP BY month ORDER BY month"
        )
        return [(row["month"], float(row["total"] or 0)) for row in rows]

    def risk_distribution(self) -> list[tuple[str, int]]:
        counts = {level: 0 for level in RISK_LEVELS}
        for item in self.exposure():
            counts[item["zone_risk"]] = counts.get(item["zone_risk"], 0) + 1
        return [(level, counts[level]) for level in RISK_LEVELS]

    def run_flood_scan(self, username: str) -> list[str]:
        created: list[str] = []
        now = datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT)
        settings = self.settings()  # fetch once
        threshold = {"info": 0, "warning": 1, "critical": 2}.get(
            settings.get("alert_threshold", "info"), 0
        )
        severity_rank = {"info": 0, "warning": 1, "critical": 2}
        # Fetch exposure ONCE — it already batches the zones query.
        exposure = self.exposure()
        # Pre-fetch all open alert titles in one query so the loop does not
        # hit the database once per exposed vehicle.
        open_titles = {
            row["title"]
            for row in self.rows(
                "SELECT title FROM alerts WHERE status != 'resolved' AND source = 'flood scan'"
            )
        }
        for item in exposure:
            if not item["inside"]:
                continue
            severity = "critical" if item["zone_risk"] == "severe" else "warning"
            if severity_rank.get(severity, 0) < threshold:
                continue
            title = f"Flood scan: {item['plate_number']} exposed"
            if title in open_titles:
                continue
            open_titles.add(title)
            message = (
                f"{item['brand']} {item['model']} is {item['distance_km']:.2f} km"
                f" from {item['zone_name']}"
                f" ({item['zone_risk']} risk). Recommend moving the unit before the next rainfall."
            )
            self.execute(
                "INSERT INTO alerts"
                " (title, message, severity, source, vehicle_id, status,"
                " created_by, created_at, updated_at)"
                " VALUES (?,?,?,?,?, 'new', ?, ?, ?)",
                (
                    title,
                    message,
                    severity,
                    "flood scan",
                    item["id"],
                    username,
                    now,
                    now,
                ),
            )
            self.audit(username, "flood_scan_alert", "vehicle", int(item["id"]), title)
            created.append(title)
        return created

    def raise_alert(
        self,
        title: str,
        message: str,
        severity: str = "info",
        source: str = "manual",
        vehicle_id: int | None = None,
        zone_id: int | None = None,
        username: str = "",
    ) -> None:
        now = datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT)
        self.execute(
            "INSERT INTO alerts"
            " (title, message, severity, source, vehicle_id, zone_id, status,"
            " created_by, created_at, updated_at)"
            " VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?, 'new', ?, ?, ?)",
            (title, message, severity, source, vehicle_id, zone_id, username, now, now),
        )

    def open_alert_counts(self) -> dict[str, int]:
        counts: dict[str, int] = {status: 0 for status in ALERT_STATUSES}
        for row in self.rows("SELECT status, COUNT(*) AS n FROM alerts GROUP BY status"):
            counts[row["status"]] = int(row["n"])
        return counts  # already a single GROUP BY — kept for clarity

    def set_alert_status(self, alert_id: int, status: str) -> None:
        now = datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT)
        self.execute(
            "UPDATE alerts SET status = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?", (status, now, alert_id)
        )

    def set_zone_active(self, zone_id: int, active: bool, actor: str = "system") -> None:
        now = datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT)
        self.execute(
            "UPDATE flood_zones SET is_active = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
            (1 if active else 0, now, zone_id),
        )
        self._bust_zones_cache()
        self.audit(
            actor, "zone_toggle", "flood_zone", zone_id, f"active={'yes' if active else 'no'}"
        )

    # -- customer management ----------------------------------------------
    def add_customer(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
        now = datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT)
        name = str(data.get("full_name") or "").strip()
        phone = str(data.get("phone") or "").strip()
        if not name:
            raise ValueError("The customer needs a full name.")
        if not phone:
            raise ValueError("The customer needs at least one contact number.")
        if not valid_phone(phone):
            raise ValueError("Enter a valid contact number (digits, spaces, +, -, parentheses).")
        email = str(data.get("email") or "").strip()
        if email and not valid_email(email):
            raise ValueError("Enter a valid e-mail address or leave it blank.")
        customer_id = self.execute(
            "INSERT INTO customers"
            " (full_name, phone, email, id_type, id_number, license_number,"
            " address, latitude, longitude, notes, created_at, updated_at)"
            " VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
            (
                name,
                phone,
                str(data.get("email") or "").strip() or None,
                data.get("id_type") or "Drivers License",
                str(data.get("id_number") or "").strip() or None,
                str(data.get("license_number") or "").strip() or None,
                str(data.get("address") or "").strip() or None,
                data.get("latitude"),
                data.get("longitude"),
                str(data.get("notes") or "").strip() or None,
                now,
                now,
            ),
        )
        self.audit(
            str(data.get("_actor") or "system"), "customer_add", "customer", customer_id, name
        )
        return customer_id

    def update_customer(self, customer_id: int, data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        customer = self.row("SELECT * FROM customers WHERE id = ?", (customer_id,))
        if customer is None:
            raise ValueError("That customer no longer exists.")
        name = str(data.get("full_name") or "").strip()
        phone = str(data.get("phone") or "").strip()
        if not name:
            raise ValueError("The customer needs a full name.")
        if not phone:
            raise ValueError("The customer needs at least one contact number.")
        if not valid_phone(phone):
            raise ValueError("Enter a valid contact number (digits, spaces, +, -, parentheses).")
        email = str(data.get("email") or "").strip()
        if email and not valid_email(email):
            raise ValueError("Enter a valid e-mail address or leave it blank.")
        latitude = data.get("latitude", customer["latitude"])
        longitude = data.get("longitude", customer["longitude"])
        now = datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT)
        self.execute(
            "UPDATE customers SET full_name = ?, phone = ?, email = ?,"
            " id_type = ?, id_number = ?, license_number = ?, address = ?,"
            " latitude = ?, longitude = ?, notes = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
            (
                name,
                phone,
                str(data.get("email") or "").strip() or None,
                data.get("id_type") or "Drivers License",
                str(data.get("id_number") or "").strip() or None,
                str(data.get("license_number") or "").strip() or None,
                str(data.get("address") or "").strip() or None,
                latitude,
                longitude,
                str(data.get("notes") or "").strip() or None,
                now,
                customer_id,
            ),
        )
        self.audit(
            str(data.get("_actor") or "system"), "customer_edit", "customer", customer_id, name
        )

    def delete_customer(self, customer_id: int, actor: str = "system") -> None:
        used = (
            self.value("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM bookings WHERE customer_id = ?", (customer_id,))
            or 0
        )
        if used:
            raise ValueError("This customer has bookings on file and cannot be deleted.")
        customer = self.row("SELECT full_name FROM customers WHERE id = ?", (customer_id,))
        self.execute("DELETE FROM customers WHERE id = ?", (customer_id,))
        self.audit(
            actor,
            "customer_delete",
            "customer",
            customer_id,
            customer["full_name"] if customer else "",
        )

    def customer_bookings(self, customer_id: int) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.rows(
            "SELECT b.*, v.plate_number, v.brand, v.model FROM bookings b"
            " JOIN vehicles v ON v.id = b.vehicle_id WHERE b.customer_id = ?"
            " ORDER BY b.start_date DESC",
            (customer_id,),
        )

    def customer_profile(self, customer_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        customer = self.row("SELECT * FROM customers WHERE id = ?", (customer_id,))
        if customer is None:
            raise ValueError("That customer no longer exists.")
        history = self.customer_bookings(customer_id)
        spend = sum(
            float(row["total_amount"] or 0) for row in history if row["status"] != "cancelled"
        )
        return {
            **customer,
            "bookings": history,
            "booking_count": len(history),
            "lifetime_value": spend,
        }

    # -- vehicle management ------------------------------------------------
    def add_vehicle(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
        now = datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT)
        plate = str(data.get("plate_number") or "").strip().upper()
        brand = str(data.get("brand") or "").strip()
        model = str(data.get("model") or "").strip()
        if not plate:
            raise ValueError("The unit needs a plate number.")
        if not brand or not model:
            raise ValueError("The unit needs both a brand and a model.")
        if self.row("SELECT id FROM vehicles WHERE plate_number = ? COLLATE NOCASE", (plate,)):
            raise ValueError(f"Plate {plate} is already in the fleet.")
        try:
            rate = max(0.0, float(data.get("rate_per_day") or 0))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            rate = 0.0
        try:
            year = int(data.get("year") or datetime.now().year)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            year = datetime.now().year
        try:
            seats = max(1, int(data.get("seats") or 4))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            seats = 4
        try:
            odometer = max(0, int(data.get("odometer") or 0))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            odometer = 0
        status = data.get("status") or "available"
        if status not in VEHICLE_STATUSES or status == "rented":
            status = "available"
        latitude = data.get("latitude")
        longitude = data.get("longitude")
        if latitude is None or longitude is None:
            latitude, longitude = MapCanvas.HOME[1], MapCanvas.HOME[0]
        vehicle_id = self.execute(
            "INSERT INTO vehicles"
            " (plate_number, brand, model, category, year, seats, rate_per_day,"
            " status, latitude, longitude, odometer, notes, created_at, updated_at)"
            " VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
            (
                plate,
                brand,
                model,
                data.get("category") or vehicle_category(model),
                year,
                seats,
                rate,
                status,
                latitude,
                longitude,
                odometer,
                str(data.get("notes") or "").strip() or None,
                now,
                now,
            ),
        )
        self.audit(
            str(data.get("_actor") or "system"),
            "vehicle_add",
            "vehicle",
            vehicle_id,
            f"{plate} {brand} {model}",
        )
        return vehicle_id

    def update_vehicle(self, vehicle_id: int, data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        vehicle = self.row("SELECT * FROM vehicles WHERE id = ?", (vehicle_id,))
        if vehicle is None:
            raise ValueError("That vehicle no longer exists.")
        plate = str(data.get("plate_number") or "").strip().upper()
        brand = str(data.get("brand") or "").strip()
        model = str(data.get("model") or "").strip()
        if not plate:
            raise ValueError("The unit needs a plate number.")
        if not brand or not model:
            raise ValueError("The unit needs both a brand and a model.")
        clash = self.row(
            "SELECT id FROM vehicles WHERE plate_number = ? COLLATE NOCASE AND id != ?",
            (plate, vehicle_id),
        )
        if clash:
            raise ValueError(f"Plate {plate} belongs to another unit.")
        latitude = data.get("latitude")
        longitude = data.get("longitude")
        if latitude is None:
            latitude = vehicle["latitude"]
        if longitude is None:
            longitude = vehicle["longitude"]
        if latitude is None or longitude is None:
            latitude, longitude = MapCanvas.HOME[1], MapCanvas.HOME[0]
        status = data.get("status") or vehicle["status"]
        if status not in VEHICLE_STATUSES:
            status = vehicle["status"]
        if status == "rented":
            ongoing = (
                self.value(
                    "SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM bookings WHERE vehicle_id = ? AND status = 'ongoing'",
                    (vehicle_id,),
                )
                or 0
            )
            if not ongoing:
                raise ValueError("Units are marked rented by starting a booking, not by hand.")
        try:
            year = int(data.get("year") or vehicle["year"])
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            year = int(vehicle["year"] or datetime.now().year)
        try:
            seats = max(1, int(data.get("seats") or vehicle["seats"]))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            seats = int(vehicle["seats"] or 4)
        try:
            rate = max(0.0, float(data.get("rate_per_day") or vehicle["rate_per_day"]))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            rate = float(vehicle["rate_per_day"] or 0)
        try:
            odometer = max(0, int(data.get("odometer") or vehicle["odometer"]))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            odometer = int(vehicle["odometer"] or 0)
        now = datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT)
        self.execute(
            "UPDATE vehicles SET plate_number = ?, brand = ?, model = ?,"
            " category = ?, year = ?, seats = ?, rate_per_day = ?, status = ?,"
            " latitude = ?, longitude = ?, odometer = ?, notes = ?,"
            " updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
            (
                plate,
                brand,
                model,
                data.get("category") or vehicle_category(model),
                year,
                seats,
                rate,
                status,
                latitude,
                longitude,
                odometer,
                str(data.get("notes") or "").strip() or None,
                now,
                vehicle_id,
            ),
        )
        self.sync_vehicle_status(vehicle_id)
        self.audit(
            str(data.get("_actor") or "system"),
            "vehicle_edit",
            "vehicle",
            vehicle_id,
            f"{plate} {brand} {model}",
        )

    def delete_vehicle(self, vehicle_id: int, actor: str = "system") -> None:
        used = (
            self.value("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM bookings WHERE vehicle_id = ?", (vehicle_id,))
            or 0
        )
        if used:
            raise ValueError(
                "This unit has bookings on file and cannot be deleted. Cancel or complete them first."
            )
        vehicle = self.row(
            "SELECT plate_number, brand, model FROM vehicles WHERE id = ?", (vehicle_id,)
        )
        self.execute("DELETE FROM vehicles WHERE id = ?", (vehicle_id,))
        if vehicle:
            self.audit(
                actor,
                "vehicle_delete",
                "vehicle",
                vehicle_id,
                f"{vehicle['plate_number']} {vehicle['brand']} {vehicle['model']}",
            )

    def vehicle_bookings(self, vehicle_id: int) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.rows(
            "SELECT b.*, c.full_name AS customer_name FROM bookings b"
            " JOIN customers c ON c.id = b.customer_id WHERE b.vehicle_id = ?"
            " ORDER BY b.start_date DESC LIMIT 6",
            (vehicle_id,),
        )

    # -- booking management -------------------------------------------------
    def next_booking_code(self) -> str:
        year = datetime.now().year
        prefix = f"BK-{year}-"
        last = self.value(
            "SELECT booking_code FROM bookings WHERE booking_code LIKE ?"
            " ORDER BY booking_code DESC LIMIT 1",
            (prefix + "%",),
        )
        try:
            number = int(str(last).rsplit("-", 1)[-1]) + 1 if last else 1
        except ValueError:
            number = 1
        return f"{prefix}{number:04d}"

    def booking_conflicts(
        self,
        vehicle_id: int,
        start: str,
        end: str,
        exclude_booking: int | None = None,
    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Active bookings of one unit whose dates overlap the given range."""
        rows = self.rows(
            "SELECT id, booking_code, start_date, end_date, status FROM bookings"
            " WHERE vehicle_id = ? AND status NOT IN ('completed', 'cancelled')"
            " AND start_date < ? AND end_date > ?",
            (vehicle_id, end, start),
        )
        if exclude_booking is not None:
            rows = [row for row in rows if int(row["id"]) != int(exclude_booking)]
        return rows

    def add_booking(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
        now = datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT)
        vehicle = self.row("SELECT * FROM vehicles WHERE id = ?", (data.get("vehicle_id"),))
        if vehicle is None:
            raise ValueError("Choose a unit for this booking.")
        customer = self.row("SELECT id FROM customers WHERE id = ?", (data.get("customer_id"),))
        if customer is None:
            raise ValueError("Choose the customer making this booking.")
        start = normalise_date(data.get("start_date"))
        end = normalise_date(data.get("end_date"))
        days = rental_days_between(start, end)
        if days is None or days < 1:
            raise ValueError("The return date must be on or after the pickup date.")
        clash = self.booking_conflicts(int(vehicle["id"]), start, end)
        if clash:
            first = clash[0]
            raise ValueError(
                f"{vehicle['plate_number']} is already booked"
                f" {first['start_date']} to {first['end_date']}"
                f" ({first['booking_code']}, {first['status']}). Pick other dates or another unit."
            )
        try:
            rate = max(0.0, float(data.get("rate_per_day") or vehicle["rate_per_day"]))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            rate = float(vehicle["rate_per_day"] or 0)
        try:
            deposit = max(0.0, float(data.get("deposit") or 0))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            deposit = 0.0
        status = data.get("status") or "pending"
        if status not in BOOKING_STATUSES:
            status = "pending"
        total = round(days * rate, 2)
        booking_id = self.execute(
            "INSERT INTO bookings"
            " (booking_code, vehicle_id, customer_id, start_date, end_date,"
            " pickup_location, destination, rental_days, rate_per_day,"
            " total_amount, deposit, pickup_latitude, pickup_longitude, status,"
            " notes, created_at, updated_at)"
            " VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
            (
                self.next_booking_code(),
                int(vehicle["id"]),
                int(customer["id"]),
                start,
                end,
                str(data.get("pickup_location") or "").strip() or None,
                str(data.get("destination") or "").strip() or None,
                days,
                rate,
                total,
                deposit,
                data.get("pickup_latitude"),
                data.get("pickup_longitude"),
                status,
                str(data.get("notes") or "").strip() or None,
                now,
                now,
            ),
        )
        self.sync_vehicle_status(int(vehicle["id"]))
        self.audit(
            str(data.get("_actor") or "system"),
            "booking_add",
            "booking",
            booking_id,
            f"{start}->{end} vehicle={vehicle['plate_number']}",
        )
        return booking_id

    def update_booking(self, booking_id: int, data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        booking = self.row("SELECT * FROM bookings WHERE id = ?", (booking_id,))
        if booking is None:
            raise ValueError("That booking no longer exists.")
        vehicle_id = int(data.get("vehicle_id") or booking["vehicle_id"])
        if self.row("SELECT id FROM vehicles WHERE id = ?", (vehicle_id,)) is None:
            raise ValueError("Choose a unit for this booking.")
        customer_id = int(data.get("customer_id") or booking["customer_id"])
        if self.row("SELECT id FROM customers WHERE id = ?", (customer_id,)) is None:
            raise ValueError("Choose the customer making this booking.")
        start = normalise_date(data.get("start_date") or booking["start_date"])
        end = normalise_date(data.get("end_date") or booking["end_date"])
        days = rental_days_between(start, end)
        if days is None or days < 1:
            raise ValueError("The return date must be on or after the pickup date.")
        clash = self.booking_conflicts(vehicle_id, start, end, exclude_booking=booking_id)
        if clash:
            first = clash[0]
            raise ValueError(
                f"{first['booking_code']} already holds this unit"
                f" {first['start_date']} to {first['end_date']}"
                f" ({first['status']}). Pick other dates or another unit."
            )
        try:
            rate = max(0.0, float(data.get("rate_per_day") or booking["rate_per_day"]))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            rate = float(booking["rate_per_day"] or 0)
        try:
            deposit = max(0.0, float(data.get("deposit") or booking["deposit"]))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            deposit = float(booking["deposit"] or 0)
        status = data.get("status") or booking["status"]
        if status not in BOOKING_STATUSES:
            status = booking["status"]
        now = datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT)
        self.execute(
            "UPDATE bookings SET customer_id = ?, vehicle_id = ?, start_date = ?,"
            " end_date = ?, pickup_location = ?, destination = ?, rental_days = ?,"
            " rate_per_day = ?, total_amount = ?, deposit = ?, pickup_latitude = ?,"
            " pickup_longitude = ?, status = ?, notes = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
            (
                customer_id,
                vehicle_id,
                start,
                end,
                str(data.get("pickup_location") or "").strip() or None,
                str(data.get("destination") or "").strip() or None,
                days,
                rate,
                round(days * rate, 2),
                deposit,
                data.get("pickup_latitude"),
                data.get("pickup_longitude"),
                status,
                str(data.get("notes") or "").strip() or None,
                now,
                booking_id,
            ),
        )
        self.sync_vehicle_status(vehicle_id)
        if vehicle_id != int(booking["vehicle_id"]):
            self.sync_vehicle_status(int(booking["vehicle_id"]))
        self.audit(
            str(data.get("_actor") or "system"),
            "booking_edit",
            "booking",
            booking_id,
            f"{start}->{end}",
        )

    def cancel_booking(self, booking_id: int, actor: str = "system") -> None:
        booking = self.row("SELECT * FROM bookings WHERE id = ?", (booking_id,))
        if booking is None:
            raise ValueError("That booking no longer exists.")
        if booking["status"] in ("completed", "cancelled"):
            raise ValueError(f"Booking {booking['booking_code']} is already {booking['status']}.")
        now = datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT)
        self.execute(
            "UPDATE bookings SET status = 'cancelled', updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
            (now, booking_id),
        )
        self.sync_vehicle_status(int(booking["vehicle_id"]), exclude_booking=booking_id)
        self.audit(actor, "booking_cancel", "booking", booking_id, booking["booking_code"])

    BOOKING_TRANSITIONS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
        "pending": ("confirmed", "ongoing", "cancelled"),
        "confirmed": ("ongoing", "cancelled"),
        "ongoing": ("completed", "cancelled"),
        "completed": (),
        "cancelled": (),
    }

    def set_booking_status(self, booking_id: int, status: str, actor: str = "system") -> None:
        """Move a booking along the rental workflow with transition guards."""
        if status not in BOOKING_STATUSES:
            raise ValueError("That booking status is not recognised.")
        booking = self.row("SELECT * FROM bookings WHERE id = ?", (booking_id,))
        if booking is None:
            raise ValueError("That booking no longer exists.")
        current = str(booking["status"])
        if status == current:
            return
        if status not in self.BOOKING_TRANSITIONS.get(current, ()):
            raise ValueError(f"A {current} booking cannot move to {status}.")
        self.execute(
            "UPDATE bookings SET status = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
            (status, datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT), booking_id),
        )
        self.sync_vehicle_status(int(booking["vehicle_id"]))
        self.audit(
            str(actor or "system"),
            "booking_status",
            "booking",
            booking_id,
            f"{current} -> {status}",
        )

    def extend_booking(self, booking_id: int, new_end_date: str, actor: str = "system") -> int:
        booking = self.row("SELECT * FROM bookings WHERE id = ?", (booking_id,))
        if booking is None:
            raise ValueError("That booking no longer exists.")
        if booking["status"] in ("completed", "cancelled"):
            raise ValueError(f"Booking {booking['booking_code']} is already {booking['status']}.")
        new_end = normalise_date(new_end_date)
        days = rental_days_between(str(booking["start_date"]), new_end)
        if days is None:
            raise ValueError("Enter the new return date as YYYY-MM-DD.")
        if days < int(booking["rental_days"] or 0):
            raise ValueError(
                "The new return date would shorten the rental — edit the booking instead."
            )
        clash = self.booking_conflicts(
            int(booking["vehicle_id"]),
            str(booking["start_date"]),
            new_end,
            exclude_booking=booking_id,
        )
        if clash:
            first = clash[0]
            raise ValueError(
                f"{first['booking_code']} already holds this unit from {first['start_date']}"
                f" ({first['status']}) — the extension would overlap it."
            )
        total = round(days * float(booking["rate_per_day"] or 0), 2)
        self.execute(
            "UPDATE bookings SET end_date = ?, rental_days = ?,"
            " total_amount = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
            (
                new_end,
                days,
                total,
                datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT),
                booking_id,
            ),
        )
        self.sync_vehicle_status(int(booking["vehicle_id"]))
        self.audit(actor, "booking_extend", "booking", booking_id, f"to {new_end} ({days} days)")
        return days

    def set_vehicle_status(self, vehicle_id: int, status: str, actor: str = "system") -> None:
        if status not in VEHICLE_STATUSES:
            raise ValueError("That vehicle status is not recognised.")
        vehicle = self.row("SELECT * FROM vehicles WHERE id = ?", (vehicle_id,))
        if vehicle is None:
            raise ValueError("That vehicle no longer exists.")
        if str(vehicle["status"]) == status:
            return
        if status == "rented":
            ongoing = (
                self.value(
                    "SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM bookings WHERE vehicle_id = ? AND status = 'ongoing'",
                    (vehicle_id,),
                )
                or 0
            )
            if not ongoing:
                raise ValueError("Units are marked rented by starting a booking, not by hand.")
        self.execute(
            "UPDATE vehicles SET status = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
            (status, datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT), vehicle_id),
        )
        self.audit(
            actor, "vehicle_status", "vehicle", vehicle_id, f"{vehicle['status']} -> {status}"
        )

    def sync_vehicle_status(self, vehicle_id: int, exclude_booking: int | None = None) -> None:
        vehicle = self.row("SELECT status FROM vehicles WHERE id = ?", (vehicle_id,))
        if vehicle is None:
            return
        sql = "SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM bookings WHERE vehicle_id = ? AND status = 'ongoing'"
        params: list[Any] = [vehicle_id]
        if exclude_booking is not None:
            sql += " AND id != ?"
            params.append(exclude_booking)
        ongoing = self.value(sql, params) or 0
        now = datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT)
        if ongoing and vehicle["status"] != "rented" and vehicle["status"] != "maintenance":
            self.execute(
                "UPDATE vehicles SET status = 'rented', updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
                (now, vehicle_id),
            )
        elif not ongoing and vehicle["status"] == "rented":
            self.execute(
                "UPDATE vehicles SET status = 'available', updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
                (now, vehicle_id),
            )

    def booking_balance(self, booking_id: int) -> float:
        # Single query joins bookings and the transactions aggregate — avoids
        # the previous two round-trips (one for the booking, one for the sum).
        row = self.row(
            "SELECT b.total_amount,"
            " COALESCE((SELECT SUM(CASE WHEN t.entry_type = 'refund' THEN -t.amount ELSE t.amount END)"
            "          FROM transactions t WHERE t.booking_id = b.id), 0) AS paid"
            " FROM bookings b WHERE b.id = ?",
            (booking_id,),
        )
        if row is None:
            return 0.0
        return float(row["total_amount"] or 0) - float(row["paid"] or 0)

    # -- payments -----------------------------------------------------------
    def next_transaction_reference(self) -> str:
        prefix = f"TXN-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m')}-"
        last = self.value(
            "SELECT reference FROM transactions WHERE reference LIKE ? ORDER BY reference DESC LIMIT 1",
            (prefix + "%",),
        )
        try:
            number = int(str(last).rsplit("-", 1)[-1]) + 1 if last else 1
        except ValueError:
            number = 1
        return f"{prefix}{number:04d}"

    def record_payment(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        booking_id = int(data.get("booking_id") or 0)
        if self.row("SELECT id FROM bookings WHERE id = ?", (booking_id,)) is None:
            raise ValueError("Choose the booking this payment belongs to.")
        try:
            amount = float(data.get("amount") or 0)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            amount = 0.0
        if amount <= 0:
            raise ValueError("Enter an amount greater than zero.")
        entry_type = data.get("entry_type") or "payment"
        if entry_type not in PAYMENT_TYPES:
            raise ValueError("Choose a valid entry type.")
        # Prevent refunds that exceed the total already paid — protects against
        # double refunds and inconsistent transaction states.
        if entry_type == "refund":
            balance = self.booking_balance(booking_id)
            if amount > balance:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Refund of {money(amount)} exceeds the outstanding balance of {money(balance)}."
                )
        entry_type = data.get("entry_type") or "payment"
        if entry_type not in PAYMENT_TYPES:
            raise ValueError("Choose a valid entry type.")
        method = data.get("method") or "cash"
        if method not in PAYMENT_METHODS:
            raise ValueError("Choose a valid payment method.")
        now = datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT)
        paid_at = str(data.get("paid_at") or "").strip() or now
        try:
            paid_at = datetime.strptime(paid_at, DATETIME_FORMAT).strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT)
        except ValueError:
            raise ValueError("Enter the paid-at time as YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM.") from None
        reference = self.next_transaction_reference()
        self.execute(
            "INSERT INTO transactions"
            " (reference, booking_id, amount, method, entry_type, paid_at,"
            " recorded_by, notes, created_at)"
            " VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
            (
                reference,
                booking_id,
                amount,
                method,
                entry_type,
                paid_at,
                str(data.get("recorded_by") or "").strip() or None,
                str(data.get("notes") or "").strip() or None,
                now,
            ),
        )
        self.audit(
            str(data.get("recorded_by") or "system"),
            "payment_record",
            "transaction",
            None,
            f"{reference} {entry_type} {money(amount)}",
        )
        return reference

    # -- flood zone management ----------------------------------------------
    def add_zone(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
        now = datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT)
        name = str(data.get("name") or "").strip()
        if not name:
            raise ValueError("The flood zone needs a name.")
        # bust cache after we read inputs but before mutation
        risk = data.get("risk_level") or "moderate"
        if risk not in RISK_LEVELS:
            risk = "moderate"
        try:
            latitude = float(data["latitude"])
            longitude = float(data["longitude"])
        except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
            raise ValueError("Pin the zone on the map or enter its coordinates.") from None
        try:
            radius = max(0.1, float(data.get("radius_km") or 1.0))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            radius = 1.0
        zone_id = self.execute(
            "INSERT INTO flood_zones"
            " (name, barangay, risk_level, latitude, longitude, radius_km,"
            " is_active, notes, created_at, updated_at)"
            " VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
            (
                name,
                str(data.get("barangay") or "").strip() or None,
                risk,
                latitude,
                longitude,
                radius,
                1 if data.get("is_active", True) else 0,
                str(data.get("notes") or "").strip() or None,
                now,
                now,
            ),
        )
        self._bust_zones_cache()
        self.audit(str(data.get("_actor") or "system"), "zone_add", "flood_zone", zone_id, name)
        return zone_id

    def _bust_zones_cache(self) -> None:
        self._zones_cache = None

    def update_zone(self, zone_id: int, data: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        zone = self.row("SELECT * FROM flood_zones WHERE id = ?", (zone_id,))
        if zone is None:
            raise ValueError("That flood zone no longer exists.")
        name = str(data.get("name") or zone["name"]).strip()
        if not name:
            raise ValueError("The flood zone needs a name.")
        risk = data.get("risk_level") or zone["risk_level"]
        if risk not in RISK_LEVELS:
            risk = zone["risk_level"]
        try:
            latitude = float(data.get("latitude") or zone["latitude"])
            longitude = float(data.get("longitude") or zone["longitude"])
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            latitude, longitude = zone["latitude"], zone["longitude"]
        try:
            radius = max(0.1, float(data.get("radius_km") or zone["radius_km"]))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            radius = zone["radius_km"]
        now = datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT)
        self.execute(
            "UPDATE flood_zones SET name = ?, barangay = ?, risk_level = ?, latitude = ?, longitude = ?,"
            " radius_km = ?, is_active = ?, notes = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
            (
                name,
                str(data.get("barangay") or "").strip() or None,
                risk,
                latitude,
                longitude,
                radius,
                1 if data.get("is_active", bool(zone["is_active"])) else 0,
                str(data.get("notes") or "").strip() or None,
                now,
                zone_id,
            ),
        )
        self._bust_zones_cache()
        self.audit(str(data.get("_actor") or "system"), "zone_edit", "flood_zone", zone_id, name)

    def delete_zone(self, zone_id: int, actor: str = "system") -> None:
        zone = self.row("SELECT name FROM flood_zones WHERE id = ?", (zone_id,))
        self.execute("DELETE FROM flood_zones WHERE id = ?", (zone_id,))
        self._bust_zones_cache()
        self.audit(actor, "zone_delete", "flood_zone", zone_id, zone["name"] if zone else "")

    # -- user management -----------------------------------------------------
    def add_user(
        self, username: str, full_name: str, role: str, password: str, actor: str = "system"
    ) -> int:
        username = username.strip()
        full_name = full_name.strip()
        if len(username) < 3:
            raise ValueError("Usernames need at least 3 characters.")
        if not full_name:
            raise ValueError("Enter the account holder's full name.")
        if role not in USER_ROLES:
            raise ValueError("Choose a valid role.")
        score, label = password_strength(password)
        if score == 0:
            raise ValueError("Passwords need at least 8 characters.")
        if score < 2:
            raise ValueError(f"Password is too weak ({label}). Add uppercase, digits or symbols.")
        if self.row("SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = ? COLLATE NOCASE", (username,)):
            raise ValueError(f"The username '{username}' is already taken.")
        salt, digest = hash_password(password)
        now = datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT)
        user_id = self.execute(
            "INSERT INTO users"
            " (username, full_name, role, password_salt, password_hash,"
            " is_active, last_seen, created_at, updated_at)"
            " VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,1,NULL,?,?)",
            (username, full_name, role, salt, digest, now, now),
        )
        self.audit(actor, "user_add", "user", user_id, f"{username} ({role})")
        return user_id

    def set_user_active(self, user_id: int, active: bool, actor: str = "system") -> None:
        user = self.row("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?", (user_id,))
        if user is None:
            raise ValueError("That account no longer exists.")
        if not active:
            if user["role"] == "admin":
                others = (
                    self.value(
                        "SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM users"
                        " WHERE role = 'admin' AND is_active = 1 AND id != ?",
                        (user_id,),
                    )
                    or 0
                )
                if others == 0:
                    raise ValueError("At least one active administrator must remain.")
            if user["username"] == DEFAULT_ADMIN_USERNAME:
                raise ValueError("The built-in administrator account cannot be disabled.")
        self.execute(
            "UPDATE users SET is_active = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
            (1 if active else 0, datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT), user_id),
        )
        self.audit(actor, "user_toggle", "user", user_id, f"active={'yes' if active else 'no'}")

    def reset_password(self, user_id: int, password: str, actor: str = "system") -> None:
        score, label = password_strength(password)
        if score == 0:
            raise ValueError("Passwords need at least 8 characters.")
        if score < 2:
            raise ValueError(f"Password is too weak ({label}). Add uppercase, digits or symbols.")
        if self.row("SELECT id FROM users WHERE id = ?", (user_id,)) is None:
            raise ValueError("That account no longer exists.")
        salt, digest = hash_password(password)
        self.execute(
            "UPDATE users SET password_salt = ?, password_hash = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
            (salt, digest, datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT), user_id),
        )
        self.audit(actor, "user_password_reset", "user", user_id, "password reset")

    # -- maintenance ------------------------------------------------------
    def add_maintenance(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
        now = datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT)
        vehicle_id = int(data.get("vehicle_id") or 0)
        if self.row("SELECT id FROM vehicles WHERE id = ?", (vehicle_id,)) is None:
            raise ValueError("Choose the vehicle for this maintenance record.")
        service_type = data.get("service_type") or "other"
        if service_type not in MAINTENANCE_TYPES:
            service_type = "other"
        performed_at = str(data.get("performed_at") or "").strip() or datetime.now().strftime(
            DATE_FORMAT
        )
        try:
            performed_at = datetime.strptime(performed_at, DATE_FORMAT).strftime(DATE_FORMAT)
        except ValueError:
            raise ValueError("Enter the service date as YYYY-MM-DD.") from None
        try:
            cost = max(0.0, float(data.get("cost") or 0))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            cost = 0.0
        try:
            odometer = max(0, int(data.get("odometer") or 0))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            odometer = 0
        record_id = self.execute(
            "INSERT INTO maintenance_records"
            " (vehicle_id, service_type, performed_at, odometer, cost, provider,"
            " notes, next_due_date, next_due_odometer, created_at)"
            " VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
            (
                vehicle_id,
                service_type,
                performed_at,
                odometer,
                cost,
                str(data.get("provider") or "").strip() or None,
                str(data.get("notes") or "").strip() or None,
                str(data.get("next_due_date") or "").strip() or None,
                int(data.get("next_due_odometer") or 0) or None,
                now,
            ),
        )
        # Update vehicle odometer if the service odometer is higher
        if odometer > 0:
            current = self.value("SELECT odometer FROM vehicles WHERE id = ?", (vehicle_id,)) or 0
            if odometer > int(current or 0):
                self.execute(
                    "UPDATE vehicles SET odometer = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
                    (odometer, now, vehicle_id),
                )
        self.audit(
            str(data.get("_actor") or "system"),
            "maintenance_add",
            "maintenance",
            record_id,
            f"{service_type} vehicle={vehicle_id}",
        )
        return record_id

    def maintenance_records(self, vehicle_id: int | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        if vehicle_id is not None:
            return self.rows(
                "SELECT m.*, v.plate_number, v.brand, v.model FROM maintenance_records m"
                " JOIN vehicles v ON v.id = m.vehicle_id WHERE m.vehicle_id = ?"
                " ORDER BY m.performed_at DESC",
                (int(vehicle_id),),
            )
        return self.rows(
            "SELECT m.*, v.plate_number, v.brand, v.model FROM maintenance_records m"
            " JOIN vehicles v ON v.id = m.vehicle_id ORDER BY m.performed_at DESC"
        )

    def upcoming_maintenance(self, days: int = 30) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Maintenance records with a next_due_date within the next `days` days."""
        cutoff = (datetime.now() + timedelta(days=days)).strftime(DATE_FORMAT)
        today = datetime.now().strftime(DATE_FORMAT)
        return self.rows(
            "SELECT m.*, v.plate_number, v.brand, v.model FROM maintenance_records m"
            " JOIN vehicles v ON v.id = m.vehicle_id"
            " WHERE m.next_due_date IS NOT NULL AND m.next_due_date != ''"
            " AND m.next_due_date <= ? AND m.next_due_date >= ?"
            " ORDER BY m.next_due_date",
            (cutoff, today),
        )

    def maintenance_summary(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        total = self.value("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM maintenance_records") or 0
        this_month = (
            self.value(
                "SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM maintenance_records"
                " WHERE strftime('%Y-%m', performed_at) = strftime('%Y-%m', 'now')"
            )
            or 0
        )
        total_cost = self.value("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(cost), 0) AS n FROM maintenance_records") or 0
        upcoming = len(self.upcoming_maintenance(30))
        return {
            "total": int(total),
            "this_month": int(this_month),
            "total_cost": float(total_cost),
            "upcoming_30d": upcoming,
        }

    def delete_maintenance(self, record_id: int, actor: str = "system") -> None:
        self.execute("DELETE FROM maintenance_records WHERE id = ?", (record_id,))
        self.audit(actor, "maintenance_delete", "maintenance", record_id, "")

    # -- vehicle documents ------------------------------------------------
    def add_document(self, data: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
        now = datetime.now().strftime(DATETIME_FORMAT)
        vehicle_id = int(data.get("vehicle_id") or 0)
        if self.row("SELECT id FROM vehicles WHERE id = ?", (vehicle_id,)) is None:
            raise ValueError("Choose the vehicle for this document.")
        doc_type = data.get("doc_type") or "other"
        if doc_type not in DOCUMENT_TYPES:
            doc_type = "other"
        doc_id = self.execute(
            "INSERT INTO vehicle_documents"
            " (vehicle_id, doc_type, document_number, issued_date, expiry_date,"
            " provider, notes, created_at, updated_at)"
            " VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
            (
                vehicle_id,
                doc_type,
                str(data.get("document_number") or "").strip() or None,
                str(data.get("issued_date") or "").strip() or None,
                str(data.get("expiry_date") or "").strip() or None,
                str(data.get("provider") or "").strip() or None,
                str(data.get("notes") or "").strip() or None,
                now,
                now,
            ),
        )
        self.audit(
            str(data.get("_actor") or "system"),
            "document_add",
            "vehicle_document",
            doc_id,
            f"{doc_type} vehicle={vehicle_id}",
        )
        return doc_id

    def documents(self, vehicle_id: int | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        if vehicle_id is not None:
            return self.rows(
                "SELECT d.*, v.plate_number, v.brand, v.model FROM vehicle_documents d"
                " JOIN vehicles v ON v.id = d.vehicle_id WHERE d.vehicle_id = ? ORDER BY d.expiry_date",
                (int(vehicle_id),),
            )
        return self.rows(
            "SELECT d.*, v.plate_number, v.brand, v.model FROM vehicle_documents d"
            " JOIN vehicles v ON v.id = d.vehicle_id ORDER BY d.expiry_date"
        )

    def expiring_documents(self, days: int = 30) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        cutoff = (datetime.now() + timedelta(days=days)).strftime(DATE_FORMAT)
        today = datetime.now().strftime(DATE_FORMAT)
        return self.rows(
            "SELECT d.*, v.plate_number, v.brand, v.model FROM vehicle_documents d"
            " JOIN vehicles v ON v.id = d.vehicle_id"
            " WHERE d.expiry_date IS NOT NULL AND d.expiry_date != ''"
            " AND d.expiry_date <= ? AND d.expiry_date >= ?"
            " ORDER BY d.expiry_date",
            (cutoff, today),
        )

    def delete_document(self, doc_id: int, actor: str = "system") -> None:
        self.execute("DELETE FROM vehicle_documents WHERE id = ?", (doc_id,))
        self.audit(actor, "document_delete", "vehicle_document", doc_id, "")

    def backup(self, destination: str) -> Path:
        target = Path(destination)
        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with self.connect() as connection:
            backup_connection = sqlite3.connect(str(target))
            try:
                connection.backup(backup_connection)
            finally:
                backup_connection.close()
        return target

    # -- reports ----------------------------------------------------------
    REPORTS: dict[str, tuple[str, str]] = {
        "vehicles": ("Vehicle inventory", "Plate, category, seats, rate and status"),
        "customers": ("Customer directory", "Contact details and identification"),
        "bookings": ("Booking ledger", "Periods, destinations, deposits and totals"),
        "transactions": ("Cash and payments", "Payments, deposits and refunds by method"),
        "flood": ("Flood risk assessment", "Active zones, severity and exposed units"),
        "alerts": ("Alert log", "Every notice raised, its source and status"),
        "maintenance": ("Maintenance history", "Service records, costs and upcoming due dates"),
        "utilization": ("Fleet utilization", "Booking counts and revenue per vehicle"),
        "customer_activity": (
            "Customer activity",
            "Lifetime value and booking counts per customer",
        ),
        "revenue": ("Revenue analytics", "Monthly net cash movement"),
        "audit": ("Audit log", "Every security-relevant action with timestamp and user"),
    }

    def report(self, key: str) -> tuple[list[str], list[list[Any]]]:
        if key == "vehicles":
            return ["Plate", "Unit", "Category", "Seats", "Rate", "Status"], [
                [
                    row["plate_number"],
                    f"{row['brand']} {row['model']}",
                    row["category"],
                    row["seats"],
                    money(row["rate_per_day"]),
                    row["status"],
                ]
                for row in self.vehicles()
            ]
        if key == "customers":
            return ["Name", "Phone", "E-mail", "ID type", "ID number"], [
                [
                    row["full_name"],
                    row["phone"],
                    row["email"] or "",
                    row["id_type"],
                    row["id_number"] or "",
                ]
                for row in self.customers()
            ]
        if key == "bookings":
            return [
                "Code",
                "Customer",
                "Unit",
                "Start",
                "End",
                "Days",
                "Deposit",
                "Total",
                "Status",
            ], [
                [
                    row["booking_code"],
                    row["customer_name"],
                    f"{row['brand']} {row['model']}",
                    row["start_date"],
                    row["end_date"],
                    row["rental_days"],
                    money(row["deposit"]),
                    money(row["total_amount"]),
                    row["status"],
                ]
                for row in self.bookings()
            ]
        if key == "transactions":
            return ["Reference", "Booking", "Type", "Method", "Paid at", "Recorded by", "Amount"], [
                [
                    row["reference"],
                    row["booking_code"] or "",
                    row["entry_type"],
                    row["method"],
                    row["paid_at"],
                    row["recorded_by"] or "",
                    money(row["amount"]),
                ]
                for row in self.transactions()
            ]
        if key == "flood":
            exposure = self.exposure()  # scored once, not once per zone
            return ["Zone", "Barangay", "Risk", "Radius km", "Active", "Exposed units"], [
                [
                    row["name"],
                    row["barangay"] or "",
                    row["risk_level"],
                    f"{row['radius_km']:.1f}",
                    "yes" if row["is_active"] else "no",
                    sum(
                        1
                        for item in exposure
                        if item["zone_name"] == row["name"] and item["inside"]
                    ),
                ]
                for row in self.zones()
            ]
        if key == "maintenance":
            return [
                "Vehicle",
                "Service",
                "Performed at",
                "Odometer",
                "Cost",
                "Provider",
                "Next due",
            ], [
                [
                    f"{row['plate_number']} {row['brand']} {row['model']}",
                    row["service_type"],
                    row["performed_at"],
                    f"{row['odometer']:,}" if row["odometer"] else "",
                    money(row["cost"]),
                    row["provider"] or "",
                    row["next_due_date"] or "",
                ]
                for row in self.maintenance_records()
            ]
        if key == "utilization":
            return ["Plate", "Unit", "Bookings", "Revenue"], [
                [
                    row["plate_number"],
                    f"{row['brand']} {row['model']}",
                    row["booking_count"],
                    money(row["revenue"]),
                ]
                for row in self.top_vehicles(limit=100)
            ]
        if key == "customer_activity":
            return ["Name", "Phone", "Bookings", "Lifetime value"], [
                [row["full_name"], row["phone"], row["booking_count"], money(row["lifetime_value"])]
                for row in self.top_customers(limit=100)
            ]
        if key == "revenue":
            return ["Month", "Net cash"], [
                [month, money(total)] for month, total in self.monthly_revenue()
            ]
        if key == "audit":
            return ["When", "User", "Action", "Entity", "Details"], [
                [
                    row["created_at"],
                    row["username"],
                    row["action"],
                    f"{row['entity_type']}#{row['entity_id']}" if row["entity_id"] else "",
                    row["details"],
                ]
                for row in self.audit_logs(limit=500)
            ]
        return ["When", "Title", "Severity", "Source", "Status"], [
            [row["created_at"], row["title"], row["severity"], row["source"], row["status"]]
            for row in self.alerts()
        ]

# Record dialogs — Customer / Vehicle / Zone / Booking / Payment / User

class PinMapCanvas(MapCanvas):
    """Map canvas with one editable marker, used by the record dialogs."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        master: tk.Widget,
        store: "Store",
        pin: tuple[float, float] | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        self.pin = None  # set before super().__init__ because MapCanvas draws on creation
        kwargs.setdefault("show_vehicles", False)
        kwargs.setdefault("show_grid", False)
        kwargs.setdefault("legend", False)
        super().__init__(master, store, **kwargs)
        # pin maps start closer to street level; recentre on an existing pin
        self.span_km = 9.0
        if pin is not None:
            self.center_lat, self.center_lon = pin[0], pin[1]
        self.pin = pin
        self.draw()

    def draw(self) -> None:
        super().draw()
        if self.pin is None:
            return
        x, y = self.project(*self.pin)
        self.create_oval(
            x - 10,
            y - 10,
            x + 10,
            y + 10,
            fill=_mix(PALETTE["accent"], "#FFFFFF", 0.55),
            outline="",
        )
        self.create_oval(
            x - 6, y - 6, x + 6, y + 6, fill=PALETTE["accent"], outline="#FFFFFF", width=2
        )

    def set_pin(self, position: tuple[float, float] | None) -> None:
        self.pin = position
        self.draw()


class FormDialog(tk.Toplevel):
    """Modal card window: heading, two-column fields, optional map pin, save/cancel."""

    def __init__(self, parent: tk.Widget, title: str, subtitle: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.result = False
        self.fields: dict[str, Field] = {}
        self.checks: dict[str, tk.BooleanVar] = {}
        self._row = 0
        self._col = 0
        self.pin_map: PinMapCanvas | None = None
        self.pin_text = tk.StringVar(value="none")
        self.title(title)
        self.configure(background=PALETTE["bg"], padx=18, pady=18)
        self.resizable(False, False)
        self.transient(parent.winfo_toplevel())
        card = ttk.Frame(self, style="CardBorder.TFrame", padding=1)
        card.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        self.body = ttk.Frame(card, style="Card.TFrame", padding=(32, 28))
        self.body.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        # header — title and subtitle share a tight block, separated from the
        # form below by a quiet hairline so the dialog reads as one card
        header = ttk.Frame(self.body, style="Card.TFrame")
        header.pack(fill="x")
        ttk.Label(header, text=title, style="HeadingCard.TLabel").pack(anchor="w")
        ttk.Label(
            header,
            text=subtitle,
            style="MutedCard.TLabel",
            wraplength=round(460 * ui_scale()),
            justify="left",
        ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(3, 0))
        ttk.Frame(self.body, style="CardBorder.TFrame", height=1).pack(fill="x", pady=(14, 0))
        self.form_canvas = tk.Canvas(
            self.body, background=PALETTE["surface"], highlightthickness=0, bd=0
        )
        self.form_canvas.pack(fill="both", expand=True, pady=(14, 0))
        # The field area lives in a scrolling canvas: when a dialog would
        # otherwise outgrow the screen (easy at 125-150% Windows scaling)
        # the header and footer stay pinned and only the fields scroll.
        self.form = ttk.Frame(self.form_canvas, style="Card.TFrame")
        self._form_window = self.form_canvas.create_window((0, 0), window=self.form, anchor="nw")
        self.form.bind(
            "<Configure>",
            lambda _event: self.form_canvas.configure(scrollregion=self.form_canvas.bbox("all")),
        )
        self.form_canvas.bind(
            "<Configure>",
            lambda event: self.form_canvas.itemconfigure(self._form_window, width=event.width),
        )
        self._form_scroller = WheelScroller(self.form_canvas, self.form)
        self.bind("<Destroy>", lambda _event: self._form_scroller.destroy(), add="+")
        self.error = tk.StringVar(value="")
        ttk.Frame(self.body, style="CardBorder.TFrame", height=1).pack(fill="x", pady=(18, 0))
        footer = ttk.Frame(self.body, style="Card.TFrame")
        footer.pack(fill="x", pady=(14, 0))
        self.error_label = ttk.Label(
            footer,
            textvariable=self.error,
            style="DangerCard.TLabel",
            wraplength=round(320 * ui_scale()),
            justify="left",
        )
        self.error_label.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)
        buttons = ttk.Frame(footer, style="Card.TFrame")
        buttons.pack(side="right")
        Button(buttons, text="Cancel", style="Secondary.TButton", command=self.destroy).pack(
            side="left", padx=(0, 10)
        )
        Button(buttons, text="Save", style="Primary.TButton", command=self.save).pack(side="left")
        self.bind("<Return>", lambda _event: self.save())
        self.bind("<Escape>", lambda _event: self.destroy())
        self.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", self.destroy)
        self.grab_set()

    # -- form building -----------------------------------------------------
    def _wide_row(self) -> None:
        if self._col == 1:
            self._row += 1
            self._col = 0

    def add_field(
        self,
        key: str,
        label: str,
        value: str = "",
        choices: Sequence[str] | None = None,
        secret: bool = False,
        editable: bool = False,
        wide: bool = False,
        on_change: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
    ) -> Field:
        if wide:
            self._wide_row()
        field = Field(
            self.form,
            label,
            value=value,
            choices=choices,
            secret=secret,
            editable=editable,
            on_change=on_change,
        )
        column = 0 if wide else self._col
        field.grid(
            row=self._row,
            column=column,
            columnspan=2 if wide else 1,
            sticky="ew",
            padx=(0 if column == 0 else 12, 0),
            pady=(0, 10),
        )
        if wide or self._col == 1:
            self._row += 1
            self._col = 0
        else:
            self._col = 1
        self.form.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
        self.form.columnconfigure(1, weight=1)
        self.fields[key] = field
        return field

    def add_check(self, key: str, label: str, checked: bool) -> tk.BooleanVar:
        self._wide_row()
        variable = tk.BooleanVar(value=checked)
        box = ttk.Frame(self.form, style="Card.TFrame")
        box.grid(row=self._row, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="w", pady=(0, 10))
        ttk.Checkbutton(box, text=label, variable=variable, style="Switch.TCheckbutton").pack(
            anchor="w"
        )
        self._row += 1
        self.checks[key] = variable
        return variable

    def add_summary(self) -> ttk.Label:
        self._wide_row()
        label = ttk.Label(self.form, text="", style="AccentCard.TLabel")
        label.grid(row=self._row, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="w", pady=(0, 2))
        self._row += 1
        return label

    def add_pin_map(self, store: "Store", label: str, lat: float | None, lon: float | None) -> None:
        self._wide_row()
        pin = (float(lat), float(lon)) if lat is not None and lon is not None else None
        box = ttk.Frame(self.form, style="Card.TFrame")
        box.grid(row=self._row, column=0, columnspan=2, sticky="ew", pady=(2, 10))
        head = ttk.Frame(box, style="Card.TFrame")
        head.pack(fill="x")
        ttk.Label(head, text=label.upper(), style="MicroCard.TLabel").pack(side="left")
        ttk.Label(head, textvariable=self.pin_text, style="MutedCard.TLabel").pack(
            side="left", padx=(10, 0)
        )
        Button(
            head, text="Clear pin", style="Ghost.TButton", command=lambda: self.set_pin(None)
        ).pack(side="right")
        self.pin_map = PinMapCanvas(
            box,
            store,
            pin=pin,
            height=round(185 * ui_scale()),
            show_labels=True,
            show_zones=True,
            detail="medium",
        )
        self.pin_map.pack(fill="x", pady=(6, 0))
        self.pin_map.on_click = self._handle_click
        self.set_pin(pin)
        self._row += 1

    # -- pin -----------------------------------------------------------------
    def _handle_click(self, position: tuple[float, float]) -> None:
        self.set_pin(position)

    def set_pin(self, position: tuple[float, float] | None) -> None:
        if self.pin_map is not None:
            self.pin_map.set_pin(position)
        self.pin_text.set("none" if position is None else f"{position[0]:.4f}, {position[1]:.4f}")

    def pin(self) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:
        if self.pin_map is None or self.pin_map.pin is None:
            return None, None
        lat, lon = self.pin_map.pin
        return float(lat), float(lon)

    # -- saving --------------------------------------------------------------
    def value(self, key: str) -> str:
        return self.fields[key].get()

    def save(self) -> None:
        try:
            self.commit()
        except (ValueError, RuntimeError) as error:
            self.error.set(str(error))
            return
        except Exception:
            log.exception("Form commit failed unexpectedly")
            self.error.set("An unexpected error occurred. See the log file for details.")
            return
        self.result = True
        self.destroy()

    def commit(self) -> None:
        """Subclasses validate and write to the store here."""
        return

    def _centre(self) -> None:
        # Settle the packer, then size the form canvas to its content (a
