    subtitle = "Rental periods, destinations, deposits and running totals"
    search_hint = "Search code, customer or unit..."
    columns = [
        ("booking_code", "Code", 84, "w"),
        ("customer_name", "Customer", 132, "w"),
        ("unit", "Unit", 158, "w"),
        ("period", "Period", 100, "w"),
        ("rental_days", "Days", 46, "e"),
        ("total_amount", "Total", 62, "e"),
        ("status", "Status", 72, "w"),
    ]
    filters = ["all", *BOOKING_STATUSES]
    uses_detail = True
    detail_title = "Booking detail"

    def actions(self) -> list[tk.Widget]:
        return [
            Button(self, text="Export CSV", style="Secondary.TButton", command=self.export),
            Button(
                self, text="Extend booking", style="Secondary.TButton", command=self.extend_selected
            ),
            Button(
                self, text="Cancel booking", style="Secondary.TButton", command=self.cancel_selected
            ),
            Button(self, text="Edit booking", style="Quiet.TButton", command=self.edit_selected),
            Button(self, text="New booking", style="Primary.TButton", command=self.new_booking),
        ]

    def export(self) -> None:
        columns, rows = self.store.report("bookings")
        export_csv(self, "bookings", columns, rows)

    def fetch(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        rows = self.store.bookings(self.filter.get(), self.search.get())
        return [
            {
                **row,
                "_total": float(row["total_amount"] or 0),
                "unit": f"{row['brand']} {row['model']}",
                # the BK- prefix is redundant inside the bookings ledger and
                # costs the code column twenty pixels it can ill afford
                "booking_code": row["booking_code"].replace("BK-", "", 1),
                # one compact period cell instead of two ISO date columns —
                # the full range stays on the detail panel and in the CSV
                "period": short_period(row["start_date"], row["end_date"]),
                "deposit": money(row["deposit"]),
                "total_amount": money(row["total_amount"]),
            }
            for row in rows
        ]

    def tag_of(self, row: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
        return {
            "ongoing": "info",
            "confirmed": "info",
            "pending": "warn",
            "completed": "ok",
            "cancelled": "danger",
        }.get(row["status"])

    def refresh(self) -> None:
        rows = self.fetch()
        self.table.set_rows(rows, tag_of=self.tag_of)
        total = sum(row["_total"] for row in rows if row["status"] != "cancelled")
        self.counter.configure(text=f"{len(rows)} bookings · {money(total)}")
        self.render_detail()

    # -- selection ------------------------------------------------------------
    def selected_booking(self) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        key = self.table.selected()
        if not key:
            return None
        return self.store.row(
            "SELECT b.*, v.plate_number, v.brand, v.model,"
            " v.status AS vehicle_status, c.full_name AS customer_name,"
            " c.phone AS customer_phone FROM bookings b JOIN vehicles v ON v.id = b.vehicle_id"
            " JOIN customers c ON c.id = b.customer_id WHERE b.id = ?",
            (int(key),),
        )

    def on_row_select(self, _key: str) -> None:
        self.render_detail()

    def on_row_double(self, _key: str) -> None:
        self.edit_selected()

    # -- actions ---------------------------------------------------------------
    def new_booking(self) -> None:
        self.app.open_new_booking()

    def focus_query(self, query: str) -> None:
        """Pre-filter the table when another page deep-links here."""
        self.search.set(query)
        self.filter.set("all")
        self.refresh()

    def set_status(self, status: str) -> None:
        """One-click workflow step: confirm, start or finish the rental."""
        booking = self.selected_booking()
        if booking is None:
            return
        store_action(
            self,
            lambda: self.store.set_booking_status(
                int(booking["id"]), status, actor=self.app.user["username"]
            ),
            on_success=lambda: (self.refresh(), self.app.refresh_all()),
        )

    def extend_selected(self) -> None:
        booking = self.selected_booking()
        if booking is None:
            messagebox.showinfo(APP_NAME, "Select a booking to extend first.", parent=self)
            return
        if booking["status"] in ("completed", "cancelled"):
            messagebox.showinfo(
                APP_NAME,
                f"Booking {booking['booking_code']} is already {booking['status']}.",
                parent=self,
            )
            return
        safe_dialog(
            self,
            lambda: ExtendDialog(self, self.store, booking, actor=self.app.user["username"]),
            on_success=lambda: (self.refresh(), self.app.refresh_all()),
        )

    def open_customer(self) -> None:
        booking = self.selected_booking()
        if booking is None:
            return
        self.app.open_customers_for(str(booking["customer_name"]))

    def edit_selected(self) -> None:
        booking = self.selected_booking()
        if booking is None:
            messagebox.showinfo(APP_NAME, "Select a booking to edit first.", parent=self)
            return
        safe_dialog(
            self,
            lambda: BookingDialog(self, self.store, booking, actor=self.app.user["username"]),
            on_success=lambda: (self.refresh(), self.app.refresh_all()),
        )

    def cancel_selected(self) -> None:
        booking = self.selected_booking()
        if booking is None:
            messagebox.showinfo(APP_NAME, "Select a booking to cancel first.", parent=self)
            return
        if booking["status"] in ("completed", "cancelled"):
            messagebox.showinfo(
                APP_NAME,
                f"Booking {booking['booking_code']} is already {booking['status']}.",
                parent=self,
            )
            return
        if not messagebox.askyesno(
            APP_NAME,
            f"Cancel booking {booking['booking_code']} for {booking['customer_name']}?",
            parent=self,
        ):
            return
        store_action(
            self,
            lambda: self.store.cancel_booking(int(booking["id"]), actor=self.app.user["username"]),
            on_success=lambda: (self.refresh(), self.app.refresh_all()),
        )

    def record_payment(self) -> None:
        booking = self.selected_booking()
        self.app.open_payment_dialog(int(booking["id"]) if booking else None)

    def locate_unit(self) -> None:
        booking = self.selected_booking()
        if booking is None:
            return
        self.app.focus_vehicle_on_map(int(booking["vehicle_id"]))

    def locate_pickup(self) -> None:
        booking = self.selected_booking()
        if booking is None or booking.get("pickup_latitude") is None:
            return
        self.app.focus_point_on_map(
            float(booking["pickup_latitude"]), float(booking["pickup_longitude"]), span=5.0
        )

    # -- detail -----------------------------------------------------------------
    def render_detail(self) -> None:
        if not hasattr(self, "detail_body"):
            return
        for child in self.detail_body.winfo_children():
            child.destroy()
        booking = self.selected_booking()
        if booking is None:
            empty_state_block(
                self.detail_body,
                "No booking selected",
                "Select a booking to review its period, balance and actions.",
            )
            return
        ttk.Label(self.detail_body, text=booking["booking_code"], style="HeadingCard.TLabel").pack(
            anchor="w"
        )
        ttk.Label(
            self.detail_body,
            text=f"{booking['customer_name']} · {booking['customer_phone'] or 'no contact'}",
            style="MutedCard.TLabel",
            wraplength=round(230 * ui_scale()),
            justify="left",
        ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(3, 0))
        facts = ttk.Frame(self.detail_body, style="Card.TFrame")
        facts.pack(fill="x", pady=(12, 0))
        balance = self.store.booking_balance(int(booking["id"]))
        items = [
            ("Unit", f"{booking['brand']} {booking['model']}"),
            ("Plate", booking["plate_number"]),
            ("Period", f"{booking['start_date']} → {booking['end_date']}"),
            ("Days", str(booking["rental_days"])),
            ("Rate / day", money(booking["rate_per_day"])),
            ("Total", money(booking["total_amount"])),
            ("Deposit", money(booking["deposit"])),
            ("Balance", money(balance)),
        ]
        for index, (label, value) in enumerate(items):
            box = ttk.Frame(facts, style="Sunken.TFrame", padding=(10, 9))
            box.grid(row=index // 2, column=index % 2, sticky="nsew", padx=(0, 10), pady=(0, 10))
            ttk.Label(box, text=label.upper(), style="MicroSunken.TLabel").pack(anchor="w")
            ttk.Label(
                box,
                text=value,
                style="BodySunken.TLabel",
                wraplength=round(150 * ui_scale()),
                justify="left",
            ).pack(anchor="w")
        facts.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
        facts.columnconfigure(1, weight=1)
        route = []
        if booking["pickup_location"]:
            route.append(f"Pickup: {booking['pickup_location']}")
        if booking["destination"]:
            route.append(f"Destination: {booking['destination']}")
        if route:
            ttk.Label(
                self.detail_body,
                text="\n".join(route),
                style="MutedCard.TLabel",
                wraplength=round(230 * ui_scale()),
                justify="left",
            ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(4, 0))

        transitions: dict[str, list[tuple[str, str, str]]] = {
            "pending": [
                ("Confirm booking", "confirmed", "Primary.TButton"),
                ("Start rental", "ongoing", "Quiet.TButton"),
            ],
            "confirmed": [("Start rental", "ongoing", "Primary.TButton")],
            "ongoing": [("Complete rental", "completed", "Primary.TButton")],
        }
        workflow = ttk.Frame(self.detail_body, style="Card.TFrame")
        workflow.pack(fill="x", pady=(14, 0))
        for text, target, style_name in transitions.get(str(booking["status"]), []):
            Button(
                workflow,
                text=text,
                style=style_name,
                command=lambda target_status=target: self.set_status(target_status),
            ).pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))
        if not transitions.get(str(booking["status"])):
            ttk.Label(
                self.detail_body,
                text=f"This booking is {booking['status']} — no further workflow steps.",
                style="MutedCard.TLabel",
            ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(14, 0))
        buttons = ttk.Frame(self.detail_body, style="Card.TFrame")
        buttons.pack(fill="x", pady=(10, 0))
        Button(
            buttons, text="Record payment", style="Secondary.TButton", command=self.record_payment
        ).pack(side="left")
        Button(
            buttons, text="Open customer", style="Secondary.TButton", command=self.open_customer
        ).pack(side="left", padx=(8, 0))
        Button(buttons, text="Locate unit", style="Ghost.TButton", command=self.locate_unit).pack(
            side="left", padx=(8, 0)
        )
        if booking.get("pickup_latitude") is not None:
            Button(
                buttons, text="Pickup pin", style="Ghost.TButton", command=self.locate_pickup
            ).pack(side="left", padx=(8, 0))


class TransactionsPage(ListPage):
    key = "transactions"
    title = "Transactions"
    subtitle = "Payments, deposits and refunds recorded against each booking"
    search_hint = "Search reference or booking code..."
    columns = [
        ("reference", "Reference", 124, "w"),
        ("booking_code", "Booking", 108, "w"),
        ("entry_type", "Type", 92, "w"),
        ("method", "Method", 116, "w"),
        ("recorded_by", "Recorded by", 108, "w"),
        ("paid_at", "Paid at", 144, "w"),
        ("amount", "Amount", 100, "e"),
    ]
    filters = ["all", *PAYMENT_TYPES]

    def actions(self) -> list[tk.Widget]:
        return [
            Button(self, text="Export CSV", style="Secondary.TButton", command=self.export),
            Button(
                self, text="Record payment", style="Primary.TButton", command=self.record_payment
            ),
        ]

    def export(self) -> None:
        columns, rows = self.store.report("transactions")
        export_csv(self, "transactions", columns, rows)

    def record_payment(self) -> None:
        self.app.open_payment_dialog()

    def prefix(self) -> list[tk.Widget]:
        wrap = ttk.Frame(self, style="Page.TFrame")
        self.collected = Stat(wrap, "Collected", tone="ok", glyph="₱")
        self.refunded = Stat(wrap, "Refunded", tone="danger", glyph="↩")
        self.net = Stat(wrap, "Net", tone="accent", glyph="≡")
        for index, tile in enumerate((self.collected, self.refunded, self.net)):
            wrap.columnconfigure(index, weight=1, uniform="money")
            tile.grid(row=0, column=index, sticky="nsew", padx=(0 if index == 0 else 12, 0))
        return [wrap]

    def fetch(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return [
            {**row, "amount": money(row["amount"])}
            for row in self.store.transactions(self.filter.get(), self.search.get())
        ]

    def tag_of(self, row: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
        return {"payment": "ok", "deposit": "info", "refund": "danger"}.get(row["entry_type"])

    def refresh(self) -> None:
        super().refresh()
        collected = float(
            self.store.value(
                "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN entry_type != 'refund'"
                " THEN amount ELSE 0 END), 0) FROM transactions"
            )
            or 0
        )
        refunded = float(
            self.store.value(
                "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN entry_type = 'refund'"
                " THEN amount ELSE 0 END), 0) FROM transactions"
            )
            or 0
        )
        count = int(self.store.value("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM transactions") or 0)
        self.collected.set(money(collected), f"{count} entries")
        self.refunded.set(money(refunded), "Returned to renters")
        self.net.set(money(collected - refunded), "After refunds")


class UsersPage(ListPage):
    key = "users"
    title = "Users"
    subtitle = "Accounts, roles and password policy (hashes only, never plain text)"
    search_hint = "Search account or username..."
    columns = [
        ("full_name", "Account", 190, "w"),
        ("username", "Username", 130, "w"),
        ("role", "Role", 100, "w"),
        ("last_seen", "Last seen", 140, "w"),
        ("state", "State", 100, "w"),
    ]
    uses_detail = True
    detail_title = "Account"

    def actions(self) -> list[tk.Widget]:
        return [
            Button(
                self,
                text="Disable account",
                style="Secondary.TButton",
                command=self.toggle_selected,
            ),
            Button(self, text="Reset password", style="Quiet.TButton", command=self.reset_password),
            Button(self, text="Add user", style="Primary.TButton", command=self.add_user),
        ]

    def prefix(self) -> list[tk.Widget]:
        wrap = ttk.Frame(self, style="Page.TFrame")
        for index, (role, description) in enumerate(
            (
                ("Admin", "Full access, including user accounts, backups and settings."),
                ("Manager", "Fleet, bookings, transactions, zones, reports and alerts."),
                ("Staff", "Bookings, customers and vehicle lookup only."),
            )
        ):
            card = Card(wrap, padding=(16, 14))
            wrap.columnconfigure(index, weight=1, uniform="roles")
            card.grid(row=0, column=index, sticky="nsew", padx=(0 if index == 0 else 10, 0))
            ttk.Label(card.body, text=role, style="TitleCard.TLabel").pack(anchor="w")
            ttk.Label(
                card.body,
                text=description,
                style="MutedCard.TLabel",
                wraplength=round(200 * ui_scale()),
                justify="left",
            ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(4, 0))
        return [wrap]

    def fetch(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return [
            {
                **row,
                "state": "active" if row["is_active"] else "disabled",
                "last_seen": row["last_seen"] or "never",
            }
            for row in self.store.users()
        ]

    def tag_of(self, row: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
        return {"admin": "accent", "manager": "info", "staff": "muted"}.get(row["role"])

    # -- selection ------------------------------------------------------------
    def selected_user(self) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
        key = self.table.selected()
        if not key:
            return None
        return self.store.row("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?", (int(key),))

    def on_row_select(self, _key: str) -> None:
        self.render_detail()

    # -- actions ---------------------------------------------------------------
    def add_user(self) -> None:
        safe_dialog(
            self,
            lambda: UserDialog(self, self.store, actor=self.app.user["username"]),
            on_success=lambda: (self.refresh(), self.render_detail()),
        )

    def reset_password(self) -> None:
        user = self.selected_user()
        if user is None:
            messagebox.showinfo(APP_NAME, "Select an account first.", parent=self)
            return

        def _on_success() -> None:
            messagebox.showinfo(
                APP_NAME, f"The password for {user['username']} was reset.", parent=self
            )
            self.refresh()
            self.render_detail()

        safe_dialog(
            self,
            lambda: PasswordDialog(self, self.store, user, actor=self.app.user["username"]),
            on_success=_on_success,
        )

    def toggle_selected(self) -> None:
        user = self.selected_user()
        if user is None:
            messagebox.showinfo(APP_NAME, "Select an account first.", parent=self)
            return
        disable = bool(user["is_active"])
        verb = "Disable" if disable else "Re-enable"
        if not messagebox.askyesno(
            APP_NAME, f"{verb} the account '{user['username']}'?", parent=self
        ):
            return
        store_action(
            self,
            lambda: self.store.set_user_active(
                int(user["id"]), not disable, actor=self.app.user["username"]
            ),
            on_success=lambda: (self.refresh(), self.render_detail()),
        )

    # -- detail ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def render_detail(self) -> None:
        if not hasattr(self, "detail_body"):
            return
        for child in self.detail_body.winfo_children():
            child.destroy()
        user = self.selected_user()
        if user is None:
            ttk.Label(
                self.detail_body,
                text="No account selected",
                style="TitleCard.TLabel",
            ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(8, 0))
            return
        ttk.Label(self.detail_body, text=user["full_name"], style="HeadingCard.TLabel").pack(
            anchor="w"
        )
        ttk.Label(
            self.detail_body,
            text=f"@{user['username']} · {user['role'].title()}"
            f" · {'active' if user['is_active'] else 'disabled'}",
            style="MutedCard.TLabel",
        ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(3, 0))
        box = ttk.Frame(self.detail_body, style="Sunken.TFrame", padding=(11, 9))
        box.pack(fill="x", pady=(14, 0))
        ttk.Label(box, text="LAST SEEN", style="MicroSunken.TLabel").pack(anchor="w")
        ttk.Label(box, text=user["last_seen"] or "never signed in", style="BodySunken.TLabel").pack(
            anchor="w"
        )
        ttk.Label(self.detail_body, text="PASSWORDS", style="MicroCard.TLabel").pack(
            anchor="w", pady=(12, 2)
        )
        ttk.Label(
            self.detail_body,
            text="Passwords are stored as PBKDF2-SHA256 hashes with a random salt. "
            "Use 'Reset password' to hand a colleague a fresh temporary password.",
            style="MutedCard.TLabel",
            wraplength=round(230 * ui_scale()),
            justify="left",
        ).pack(anchor="w")


class GpsMapPage(Page):
    key = "gps"
    title = "GPS map"
    subtitle = "Fleet positions with active flood zones overlaid"

    def build(self) -> None:
        self.selected: dict[str, Any] | None = None
        heading(
            self,
            self.title,
            self.subtitle,
            [
                Button(
                    self,
                    text="Fit fleet",
                    style="Secondary.TButton",
                    command=lambda: self.map.fit_fleet(),
                ),
                Button(
                    self,
                    text="Reset view",
                    style="Secondary.TButton",
                    command=lambda: self.map.reset(),
                ),
                Button(
                    self, text="Zoom in", style="Ghost.TButton", command=lambda: self.map.zoom_in()
                ),
                Button(
                    self,
                    text="Zoom out",
                    style="Ghost.TButton",
                    command=lambda: self.map.zoom_out(),
                ),
            ],
        )
        _wrap, pair = grid_row(
            self,
            [lambda master: Card(master), lambda master: Card(master)],
            weights=[16, 7],
            gap=14,
        )
        _wrap.pack_configure(expand=True)
        map_card, side_card = pair
        card_title(map_card, "Metro Manila", "Live fleet positions with active flood zones")
        self.map = MapCanvas(
            map_card.body,
            self.store,
            height=470,
            on_select=self.select,
            border=False,
            feed=self.app.feed,
            show_bookings=True,
            show_customers=False,
            detail=self.app.store.settings().get("map_detail", "medium"),
        )
        self.map.pack(fill="both", expand=True, pady=(14, 0))
        try:
            zoom_span = {"10": 55.0, "11": 30.0, "12": 20.0, "13": 13.0, "14": 8.0}.get(
                self.app.store.settings().get("map_zoom", "13"), 13.0
            )
            self.map.span_km = zoom_span
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            pass

        ttk.Frame(map_card.body, style="CardBorder.TFrame", height=1).pack(fill="x", pady=(14, 0))
        controls = ttk.Frame(map_card.body, style="Card.TFrame")
        controls.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 0))
        self.play_button = Button(
            controls, text="Pause feed", style="Primary.TButton", command=self.toggle_feed
        )
        self.play_button.pack(side="left")
        self.speed_var = tk.StringVar(value=f"{self.app.feed.speed:g}×")
        self.speed_buttons: list[Button] = []
        for index, choice in enumerate(("1×", "4×", "16×", "60×")):
            button = Button(
                controls,
                text=choice,
                width=52,
                style="ToggleOff.TButton",
                command=lambda picked=choice: self.set_feed_speed(picked),
            )
            button.pack(side="left", padx=(12 if index == 0 else 0, 4))
            self.speed_buttons.append(button)
        self._style_speed_buttons()
        self.live_var = tk.StringVar(value=self.app.feed.status())
        ttk.Label(controls, textvariable=self.live_var, style="MutedCard.TLabel").pack(side="right")
        self.bind("<Destroy>", lambda _event: self._stop_live_poll())
        self._poll_live()

        self.show_vehicles = tk.BooleanVar(value=True)
        self.show_zones = tk.BooleanVar(value=True)
        self.show_labels = tk.BooleanVar(value=True)
        self.show_grid = tk.BooleanVar(value=True)
        self.show_bookings = tk.BooleanVar(value=True)
        self.show_customers = tk.BooleanVar(value=False)
        ttk.Frame(map_card.body, style="CardBorder.TFrame", height=1).pack(fill="x", pady=(14, 0))
        options = ttk.Frame(map_card.body, style="Card.TFrame")
        options.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 0))
        self.pills: dict[str, TogglePill] = {}
        for key, label, variable in (
            ("vehicles", "Vehicles", self.show_vehicles),
            ("zones", "Zones", self.show_zones),
            ("bookings", "Bookings", self.show_bookings),
            ("customers", "Customers", self.show_customers),
            ("labels", "Labels", self.show_labels),
            ("grid", "Grid", self.show_grid),
        ):
            pill = TogglePill(options, label, variable, self.apply_layers)
            pill.pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))
            self.pills[key] = pill

        card_title(side_card, "Fleet list")
        search_row = ttk.Frame(side_card.body, style="Card.TFrame")
        search_row.pack(fill="x", pady=(12, 0))
        self.query = tk.StringVar()
        list_entry = PlaceholderEntry(
            search_row,
            textvariable=self.query,
            placeholder="Search plate or model...",
            width=22,
            style="Field.TEntry",
        )
        list_entry.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)
        list_entry.entry.bind("<KeyRelease>", lambda _event: self._schedule_list_refresh())
        self._debounce = debounce(self)
        self.list_count = ttk.Label(search_row, text="", style="MutedCard.TLabel")
        self.list_count.pack(side="right", padx=(8, 0))
        self.listing = FleetList(side_card.body, on_select=self.select, height=248)
        self.listing.pack(fill="both", expand=True, pady=(10, 0))
        self.detail = ttk.Frame(side_card.body, style="Card.TFrame")
        self.detail.pack(fill="x", pady=(14, 0))
        self._data_seen: int | None = None
        self.refresh()

    def toggle_feed(self) -> None:
        running = self.app.feed.toggle()
        self.play_button.configure(text="Pause feed" if running else "Resume feed")
        self._update_live_status()

    def set_feed_speed(self, choice: str | None = None) -> None:
        if choice is not None:
            self.speed_var.set(choice)
        try:
            factor = float(self.speed_var.get().rstrip("×xX"))
        except ValueError:
            factor = 1.0
        self.app.feed.set_speed(factor)
        self._style_speed_buttons()
        self._update_live_status()

    def _style_speed_buttons(self) -> None:
        """Highlight the pill matching the current speed factor."""
        current = self.speed_var.get()
        for button in getattr(self, "speed_buttons", []):
            on = button.cget("text") == current
            button.configure(style="ToggleOn.TButton" if on else "ToggleOff.TButton")

    def _update_live_status(self) -> None:
        self.live_var.set(self.app.feed.status())

    def _poll_live(self) -> None:
        self._live_job = _safe_after(self, 1200, self._poll_live_tick, what="gps live poll")

    def _poll_live_tick(self) -> None:
        if not self.winfo_exists():
            return
        try:
            self._update_live_status()
            if self.selected is not None:
                if getattr(self, "_detail_values", None):
                    self._update_detail_values()
                else:
                    self.render_detail()
        except tk.TclError:
            return  # widget torn down between ticks
        self._poll_live()

    def _stop_live_poll(self) -> None:
        job = getattr(self, "_live_job", None)
        if job is not None:
            _safe_call(lambda: self.after_cancel(job), what="cancel live poll")
            self._live_job = None

    def _schedule_list_refresh(self) -> None:
        self._debounce(self.refresh)

    def apply_layers(self) -> None:
        self.map.show_vehicles = self.show_vehicles.get()
        self.map.show_zones = self.show_zones.get()
        self.map.show_labels = self.show_labels.get()
        self.map.show_grid = self.show_grid.get()
        self.map.show_bookings = self.show_bookings.get()
        self.map.show_customers = self.show_customers.get()
        self.map.reload()

    # -- cross-page focus hooks ------------------------------------------------
    def focus_vehicle(self, vehicle_id: int) -> None:
        self.show_vehicles.set(True)
        self.pills["vehicles"].sync()
        self.apply_layers()
        self.select(vehicle_id)
        self.map.focus(vehicle_id)

    def focus_customer(self, customer_id: int) -> None:
        self.show_customers.set(True)
        self.pills["customers"].sync()
        self.apply_layers()
        customer = self.store.row("SELECT * FROM customers WHERE id = ?", (customer_id,))
        if (
            customer is not None
            and customer.get("latitude") is not None
            and customer.get("longitude") is not None
        ):
            self.map.fly_to(float(customer["longitude"]), float(customer["latitude"]), 6.0)

    def focus_point(self, lat: float, lon: float, span: float = 5.0) -> None:
        self.map.fly_to(lon, lat, span)

    def select(self, vehicle_id: int) -> None:
        vehicle = self.store.row("SELECT * FROM vehicles WHERE id = ?", (vehicle_id,))
        if not vehicle:
            return
        self.selected = vehicle
        self.listing.set_selected(int(vehicle_id))
        self.map.focus(vehicle_id)
        self.render_detail()

    def render_detail(self) -> None:
        for child in self.detail.winfo_children():
            child.destroy()
        self._detail_values: dict[str, ttk.Label] = {}
        vehicle = self.selected
        if not vehicle:
            self._render_detail_placeholder()
            return
        has_fix = vehicle["latitude"] is not None and vehicle["longitude"] is not None
        found = (
            self.store.nearest_zone(vehicle["latitude"], vehicle["longitude"]) if has_fix else None
        )
        live = self.app.feed.live(int(vehicle["id"])) if self.app.feed is not None else None
        lat = live.lat if live else vehicle["latitude"]
        lon = live.lon if live else vehicle["longitude"]
        if live is not None and not live.parked:
            heading_value = f"{compass_text(live.heading)} {live.heading:.0f}°"
            last_fix = "live feed"
        else:
            heading_value = "stationary"
            last_fix = str(vehicle.get("updated_at") or "—")
        head = ttk.Frame(self.detail, style="Card.TFrame")
        head.pack(fill="x")
        ttk.Label(
            head, text=f"{vehicle['brand']} {vehicle['model']}", style="TitleCard.TLabel"
        ).pack(anchor="w")
        plate_row = ttk.Frame(head, style="Card.TFrame")
        plate_row.pack(anchor="w", pady=(2, 8))
        ttk.Label(plate_row, text=vehicle["plate_number"], style="BodyCard.TLabel").pack(
            side="left"
        )
        status = str(vehicle["status"])
        pill_style = {
            "available": "PillAccent",
            "rented": "PillInfo",
            "maintenance": "PillWarning",
        }.get(status, "PillNeutral")
        ttk.Label(plate_row, text=status.upper(), style=f"{pill_style}Card.TLabel").pack(
            side="left", padx=(8, 0)
        )
        for key, label, value in (
            ("lat", "Latitude", coordinate_text(lat)),
            ("lon", "Longitude", coordinate_text(lon)),
            ("heading", "Heading", heading_value),
            ("fix", "Last fix", last_fix),
        ):
            row = ttk.Frame(self.detail, style="Card.TFrame")
            row.pack(fill="x", pady=2)
            ttk.Label(row, text=label, style="MutedCard.TLabel").pack(side="left")
            value_label = ttk.Label(row, text=value, style="BodyCard.TLabel")
            value_label.pack(side="right")
            self._detail_values[key] = value_label
        if found:
            zone, kilometres = found
            ttk.Label(self.detail, text="NEAREST ZONE", style="MicroCard.TLabel").pack(
                anchor="w", pady=(12, 0)
            )
            ttk.Label(self.detail, text=zone["name"], style="TitleCard.TLabel").pack(anchor="w")
            ttk.Label(
                self.detail,
                text=f"{kilometres:.2f} km · radius {zone['radius_km']:.1f} km · {zone['risk_level']}",
                style="MutedCard.TLabel",
            ).pack(anchor="w")
        buttons = ttk.Frame(self.detail, style="Card.TFrame")
        buttons.pack(fill="x", pady=(14, 0))
        Button(
            buttons,
            text="New booking",
            style="Primary.TButton",
            command=lambda: self.app.open_new_booking(vehicle=self.selected),
        ).pack(side="left")
        Button(buttons, text="Edit unit", style="Secondary.TButton", command=self.edit_unit).pack(
            side="left", padx=(8, 0)
        )
        Button(
            buttons,
            text="Fleet list",
            style="Secondary.TButton",
            command=lambda: self.app.show_page("vehicles"),
        ).pack(side="left", padx=(8, 0))

    def _render_detail_placeholder(self) -> None:
        """Quiet how-to card shown before any unit is picked."""
        ttk.Label(self.detail, text="NO UNIT SELECTED", style="MicroCard.TLabel").pack(anchor="w")
        for line in (
            "Pick a marker on the map or a row in the fleet list",
            "to see live telemetry, flood exposure and quick actions.",
        ):
            ttk.Label(
                self.detail,
                text=line,
                style="MutedCard.TLabel",
                wraplength=round(250 * ui_scale()),
                justify="left",
            ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(2, 0))
        legend = ttk.Frame(self.detail, style="Card.TFrame")
        legend.pack(fill="x", pady=(12, 0))
        for status, color in STATUS_COLORS.items():
            row = ttk.Frame(legend, style="Card.TFrame")
            row.pack(fill="x", pady=2)
            dot = tk.Canvas(
                row, width=8, height=8, background=PALETTE["surface"], highlightthickness=0, bd=0
            )
            dot.create_oval(0, 0, 8, 8, fill=color, outline="")
            dot.pack(side="left", padx=(0, 7))
            ttk.Label(row, text=status.title(), style="MutedCard.TLabel").pack(side="left")

    def _update_detail_values(self) -> None:
        """Refresh the live telemetry labels in place, without a rebuild."""
        refs = getattr(self, "_detail_values", None)
        vehicle = self.selected
        if not refs or not vehicle:
            return
        live = self.app.feed.live(int(vehicle["id"])) if self.app.feed is not None else None
        if live is not None and not live.parked:
            heading_value = f"{compass_text(live.heading)} {live.heading:.0f}°"
            last_fix = "live feed"
        else:
            heading_value = "stationary"
            last_fix = str(vehicle.get("updated_at") or "—")
        refs["lat"].configure(text=coordinate_text(live.lat if live else vehicle["latitude"]))
        refs["lon"].configure(text=coordinate_text(live.lon if live else vehicle["longitude"]))
        refs["heading"].configure(text=heading_value)
        refs["fix"].configure(text=last_fix)

    def edit_unit(self) -> None:
        """Edit the selected unit without leaving the map."""
        if not self.selected:
            return
        safe_dialog(
            self,
            lambda: VehicleDialog(self, self.store, self.selected, actor=self.app.user["username"]),
            on_success=lambda: (self.refresh(), self.app.refresh_all()),
        )

    def refresh(self) -> None:
        self.app.feed.sync()  # pick up added / edited / deleted units immediately
        if self.selected is not None and not self.store.row(
            "SELECT id FROM vehicles WHERE id = ?", (int(self.selected["id"]),)
        ):
            self.selected = None  # the selected unit was deleted elsewhere
            self.map.select(None)
        vehicles = self.store.vehicles()
        zones = self.store.zones()  # one query for the whole listing, not one per row
        wanted = self.query.get().strip().casefold() if hasattr(self, "query") else ""
        shown = [
            vehicle
            for vehicle in vehicles
            if not wanted
            or wanted
            in (
                f"{vehicle['plate_number']} {vehicle['brand']} {vehicle['model']}"
                f" {vehicle['category']} {vehicle['status']}"
            ).casefold()
        ]
        selected_id = int(self.selected["id"]) if self.selected else None
        rows = []
        for vehicle in shown:
            found = self.store.closest_zone(zones, vehicle["latitude"], vehicle["longitude"])
            unit = self.app.feed.live(int(vehicle["id"])) if self.app.feed is not None else None
            if vehicle["latitude"] is None or vehicle["longitude"] is None:
                state = "no fix"
            elif unit is not None and not unit.parked:
                state = "moving"
            else:
                state = "parked"
            rows.append(
                {
                    "id": int(vehicle["id"]),
                    "plate": str(vehicle["plate_number"]),
                    "model": f"{vehicle['brand']} {vehicle['model']}",
                    "status": str(vehicle["status"]),
                    "state": state,
                    "right": f"{found[1]:.1f} km" if found else "",
                }
            )
        self.listing.set_rows(rows, selected_id=selected_id)
        self.list_count.configure(text=f"{len(shown)} of {len(vehicles)}")
        # Only re-read the map snapshot when the underlying data changed; a
        # keystroke in the search box then costs a cheap dynamic repaint only.
        fingerprint = (
            tuple(
                (v["id"], v["status"], v["plate_number"], v["latitude"], v["longitude"])
                for v in vehicles
            ),
            tuple((z["id"], z["is_active"], z["radius_km"], z["risk_level"]) for z in zones),
        )
        if fingerprint != getattr(self, "_data_fingerprint", None):
            self._data_fingerprint = fingerprint
            self.map.reload()
        else:
            self.map.draw()
        if self.selected:
            self.render_detail()


class FloodZonesPage(Page):
    key = "zones"
    title = "Flood zones"
    subtitle = "Monitored areas, severity scores and the units exposed to each"

    def build(self) -> None:
        heading(
            self,
            self.title,
            self.subtitle,
            [Button(self, text="Add zone", style="Primary.TButton", command=self.add_zone)],
        )
        _wrap, pair = grid_row(
            self,
            [lambda master: ScrollColumn(master, height=380), lambda master: Card(master)],
            weights=[13, 11],
            gap=14,
        )
        _wrap.pack_configure(expand=True)
        self.column, map_card = pair
        card_title(map_card, "Zone map")
        self.map = MapCanvas(
            map_card.body,
            self.store,
            height=330,
            on_select=None,
            show_grid=False,
            legend=False,
            border=False,
            feed=self.app.feed,
            detail=self.app.store.settings().get("map_detail", "medium"),
        )
        self.map.pack(fill="x", pady=(12, 0))
        scale_card = Card(map_card.body)
        scale_card.pack(fill="x", pady=(12, 0))
        card_title(scale_card, "Severity scale")
        rows = ttk.Frame(scale_card.body, style="Card.TFrame")
        rows.pack(fill="x", pady=(10, 0))
        for level in RISK_LEVELS:
            row = ttk.Frame(rows, style="Card.TFrame")
            row.pack(fill="x", pady=4)
            swatch = tk.Canvas(
                row, width=9, height=9, background=PALETTE["surface"], highlightthickness=0, bd=0
            )
            swatch.create_oval(0, 0, 9, 9, fill=RISK_COLORS[level], outline="")
            swatch.pack(side="left", padx=(0, 8))
            ttk.Label(
                row, text=f"{level.title()} risk", style="BodyCard.TLabel", width=14, anchor="w"
            ).pack(side="left")
            meter = Bar(row, color=RISK_COLORS[level])
            meter.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True, padx=(6, 8))
            meter.set(RISK_SCORES[level] / 100)
            ttk.Label(
                row, text=str(RISK_SCORES[level]), style="MutedCard.TLabel", width=4, anchor="e"
            ).pack(side="right")
        self.refresh()

    def add_zone(self) -> None:
        safe_dialog(
            self,
            lambda: ZoneDialog(self, self.store, actor=self.app.user["username"]),
            on_success=lambda: (self.refresh(), self.app.refresh_all()),
        )

    def edit_zone(self, zone_id: int) -> None:
        zone = self.store.row("SELECT * FROM flood_zones WHERE id = ?", (zone_id,))
        if zone is None:
            return
        safe_dialog(
            self,
            lambda: ZoneDialog(self, self.store, zone, actor=self.app.user["username"]),
            on_success=lambda: (self.refresh(), self.app.refresh_all()),
        )

    def delete_zone(self, zone_id: int, name: str) -> None:
        if not confirm_delete(self, f"flood zone '{name}'"):
            return
        store_action(
            self,
            lambda: self.store.delete_zone(zone_id, actor=self.app.user["username"]),
            on_success=lambda: (self.refresh(), self.app.refresh_all()),
        )

    def refresh(self) -> None:
        for child in self.column.inner.winfo_children():
            child.destroy()
        exposure = self.store.exposure()
        for zone in self.store.zones():
            card = Card(self.column.inner, padding=(18, 16))
            card.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 12))
            head = ttk.Frame(card.body, style="Card.TFrame")
            head.pack(fill="x")
            # the severity pill is packed BEFORE the title so the packer gives
            # it its full width first — a long zone name then yields instead of
            # squeezing the badge into an unreadable 'MODERA…'
            ttk.Label(
                head,
                text=zone["risk_level"].upper(),
                style=f"Pill{zone['risk_level'].capitalize()}Card.TLabel",
            ).pack(side="right")
            text = ttk.Frame(head, style="Card.TFrame")
            text.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)
            ttk.Label(text, text=zone["name"], style="TitleCard.TLabel").pack(anchor="w")
            # one quiet location line: barangay plus the pin coordinates
            location = "  ·  ".join(
                part
                for part in (
                    zone["barangay"] or "",
                    f"{zone['latitude']:.3f}, {zone['longitude']:.3f}",
                )
                if part
            )
            ttk.Label(text, text=location, style="MutedCard.TLabel").pack(anchor="w", pady=(2, 0))

            facts = ttk.Frame(card.body, style="Card.TFrame")
            facts.pack(fill="x", pady=(12, 0))
            exposed = sum(
                1 for item in exposure if item["zone_name"] == zone["name"] and item["inside"]
            )
            for index, value in enumerate(
                (f"{RISK_SCORES[zone['risk_level']]}", f"{zone['radius_km']:.1f} km", str(exposed))
            ):
                box = fact(facts, ("Score", "Radius", "Units inside")[index], value)
                box.grid(row=0, column=index, sticky="nsew", padx=(0 if index == 0 else 8, 0))
            for column in range(3):
                facts.columnconfigure(column, weight=1)

            meter = Bar(card.body, color=RISK_COLORS[zone["risk_level"]])
            meter.pack(fill="x", pady=(12, 0))
            meter.set(RISK_SCORES[zone["risk_level"]] / 100)

            if zone["notes"]:
                ttk.Label(
                    card.body,
                    text=zone["notes"],
                    style="MutedCard.TLabel",
                    wraplength=round(300 * ui_scale()),
                    justify="left",
                ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(10, 0))
            footer = ttk.Frame(card.body, style="Card.TFrame")
            footer.pack(fill="x", pady=(12, 0))
            variable = tk.BooleanVar(value=bool(zone["is_active"]))
            ttk.Checkbutton(
                footer,
                text="Active in risk scans",
                variable=variable,
                style="Switch.TCheckbutton",
                command=lambda zone_id=zone["id"], flag=variable: self.toggle(zone_id, flag.get()),
            ).pack(side="left")
            actions = ttk.Frame(footer, style="Card.TFrame")
            actions.pack(side="right")
            Button(
                actions,
                text="Edit",
                style="Secondary.TButton",
                command=lambda zone_id=zone["id"]: self.edit_zone(zone_id),
            ).pack(side="left")
            Button(
                actions,
                text="Delete",
                style="Ghost.TButton",
                command=lambda zone_id=zone["id"], zone_name=zone["name"]: self.delete_zone(
                    zone_id, zone_name
                ),
            ).pack(side="left", padx=(6, 0))
        self.map.reload()

    def toggle(self, zone_id: int, active: bool) -> None:
        store_action(
            self,
            lambda: self.store.set_zone_active(zone_id, active, actor=self.app.user["username"]),
            on_success=lambda: (self.refresh(), self.app.refresh_all()),
        )


class AlertsPage(Page):
    key = "alerts"
    title = "Alerts"
    subtitle = "Notices raised by the flood scan, by staff and by maintenance checks"

    def build(self) -> None:
        heading(
            self,
            self.title,
            self.subtitle,
            [Button(self, text="Run flood risk scan", style="Primary.TButton", command=self.scan)],
        )
        wrap = ttk.Frame(self, style="Page.TFrame")
        wrap.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 14))
        self.tiles = [
            Stat(wrap, "Unread", tone="accent", glyph="◉"),
            Stat(wrap, "Acknowledged", tone="warn", glyph="◐"),
            Stat(wrap, "Resolved", tone="ok", glyph="✓"),
            Stat(wrap, "Critical", tone="danger", glyph="⚑"),
        ]
        for index, tile in enumerate(self.tiles):
            wrap.columnconfigure(index, weight=1, uniform="alerts")
            tile.grid(row=0, column=index, sticky="nsew", padx=(0 if index == 0 else 12, 0))
        bar = ttk.Frame(self, style="Page.TFrame")
        bar.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 12))
        self.filter = tk.StringVar(value="all")
        box = ttk.Combobox(
            bar,
            textvariable=self.filter,
            values=["all", *ALERT_STATUSES],
            width=18,
            state="readonly",
            style="Field.TCombobox",
        )
        box.pack(side="left")
        box.bind("<<ComboboxSelected>>", lambda _event: self.refresh())
        self.counter = ttk.Label(bar, text="", style="Muted.TLabel")
        self.counter.pack(side="right")
        self.column = ScrollColumn(self, height=380)
        self.column.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        self.refresh()

    def scan(self) -> None:
        created = self.store.run_flood_scan(self.app.user["username"])
        self.refresh()
        self.app.refresh_all()
        if created:
            critical_now = int(
                self.store.value(
                    "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM alerts WHERE status = 'new' AND severity = 'critical'"
                )
                or 0
            )
            if self.app.store.settings().get("sound_alerts", "1") == "1" and critical_now:
                try:
                    self.winfo_toplevel().bell()
                except tk.TclError:
                    pass
            messagebox.showinfo(APP_NAME, f"{len(created)} new alert(s) raised.", parent=self)
        else:
            messagebox.showinfo(
                APP_NAME, "No vehicle currently sits inside an active flood zone.", parent=self
            )

    def refresh(self) -> None:
        alerts = self.store.alerts(self.filter.get())
        for child in self.column.inner.winfo_children():
            child.destroy()
        for alert in alerts:
            card = Card(self.column.inner, padding=(18, 15))
            card.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 10))
            head = ttk.Frame(card.body, style="Card.TFrame")
            head.pack(fill="x")
            ttk.Label(head, text=alert["title"], style="TitleCard.TLabel").pack(side="left")
            ttk.Label(
                head,
                text=alert["severity"].upper(),
                style=f"Pill{alert['severity'].capitalize()}Card.TLabel",
            ).pack(side="right")
            ttk.Label(
                card.body,
                text=alert["message"],
                style="MutedCard.TLabel",
                wraplength=round(430 * ui_scale()),
                justify="left",
            ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(6, 0))
            meta = ttk.Frame(card.body, style="Card.TFrame")
            meta.pack(fill="x", pady=(10, 0))
            ttk.Label(
                meta,
                text=f"{alert['created_at']}  ·  source {alert['source']}  ·  {alert['status']}",
                style="MicroCard.TLabel",
            ).pack(side="left")
            buttons = ttk.Frame(meta, style="Card.TFrame")
            buttons.pack(side="right")
            if alert["status"] != "resolved":
                if alert["status"] == "new":
                    Button(
                        buttons,
                        text="Acknowledge",
                        style="Secondary.TButton",
                        command=lambda alert_id=alert["id"]: self.set_status(
                            alert_id, "acknowledged"
                        ),
                    ).pack(side="left", padx=(6, 0))
                Button(
                    buttons,
                    text="Resolve",
                    style="Quiet.TButton",
                    command=lambda alert_id=alert["id"]: self.set_status(alert_id, "resolved"),
                ).pack(side="left", padx=(6, 0))
        counts = self.store.open_alert_counts()
        critical = int(
            self.store.value(
                "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM alerts WHERE severity = 'critical' AND status != 'resolved'"
            )
            or 0
        )
        self.tiles[0].set(str(counts["new"]).zfill(2), "Waiting for a response")
        self.tiles[1].set(str(counts["acknowledged"]).zfill(2), "Seen by staff")
        self.tiles[2].set(str(counts["resolved"]).zfill(2), "Closed")
        self.tiles[3].set(str(critical).zfill(2), "Needs attention now")
        self.counter.configure(text=f"{len(alerts)} notices")

    def set_status(self, alert_id: int, status: str) -> None:
        store_action(
            self,
            lambda: self.store.set_alert_status(alert_id, status),
            on_success=lambda: (self.refresh(), self.app.refresh_all()),
        )


class MaintenanceDialog(FormDialog):
    """Add or edit a maintenance record."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        parent: tk.Widget,
        store: "Store",
        record: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
        actor: str = "system",
    ) -> None:
        self.store = store
        self.record = record
        self.actor = actor
        record_data = record or {}
        super().__init__(
            parent,
            "Edit maintenance record" if record else "Add maintenance record",
            "Track oil changes, tire replacements, batteries, repairs and inspections.",
        )
        vehicles = store.vehicles()
        self.vehicle_map: dict[str, int] = {}
        for row in vehicles:
            label = f"{row['plate_number']} · {row['brand']} {row['model']}"
            self.vehicle_map[label] = int(row["id"])
        if not self.vehicle_map:
            raise ValueError("Add a vehicle before recording maintenance.")
        # pre-select the record's vehicle
        chosen = ""
        if record is not None:
            v = store.row(
                "SELECT plate_number, brand, model FROM vehicles WHERE id = ?",
                (record["vehicle_id"],),
            )
            if v:
                chosen = f"{v['plate_number']} · {v['brand']} {v['model']}"
        if not chosen:
            chosen = next(iter(self.vehicle_map))
        self.add_field("vehicle", "Vehicle", chosen, choices=sorted(self.vehicle_map), wide=True)
        self.add_field(
            "service_type",
            "Service type",
            str(record_data.get("service_type") or "oil_change"),
            choices=[s.replace("_", " ").title() for s in MAINTENANCE_TYPES],
        )
        self.add_field(
            "performed_at",
            "Service date (YYYY-MM-DD)",
            str(record_data.get("performed_at") or datetime.now().strftime(DATE_FORMAT)),
        )
        self.add_field("odometer", "Odometer (km)", str(record_data.get("odometer") or 0))
        self.add_field("cost", "Cost", plain_number(record_data.get("cost") or 0))
        self.add_field("provider", "Provider", str(record_data.get("provider") or ""), wide=True)
        self.add_field(
            "next_due_date",
            "Next due date (YYYY-MM-DD)",
            str(record_data.get("next_due_date") or ""),
            wide=True,
        )
        self.add_field("notes", "Notes", str(record_data.get("notes") or ""), wide=True)
        self._centre()

    def commit(self) -> None:
        vehicle_label = self.value("vehicle")
        if vehicle_label not in self.vehicle_map:
            raise ValueError("Choose a vehicle for this record.")
        # map the human-readable service type back to the snake_case constant
        service_label = self.value("service_type")
        service_type = "other"
        for s in MAINTENANCE_TYPES:
            if s.replace("_", " ").title() == service_label:
                service_type = s
                break
        data = {
            "vehicle_id": self.vehicle_map[vehicle_label],
            "service_type": service_type,
            "performed_at": self.value("performed_at"),
            "odometer": self.value("odometer"),
            "cost": self.value("cost"),
            "provider": self.value("provider"),
            "next_due_date": self.value("next_due_date"),
            "notes": self.value("notes"),
            "_actor": self.actor,
        }
        if self.record:
            # delete + re-add is simplest for an edit; preserves history
            self.store.delete_maintenance(int(self.record["id"]), actor=self.actor)
        self.store.add_maintenance(data)


class MaintenancePage(Page):
    """Vehicle maintenance schedule, repair history and document expiry tracking."""

    key = "maintenance"
    title = "Maintenance"
    subtitle = "Service records, upcoming reminders, registration and insurance expiry"

    def build(self) -> None:
        heading(
            self,
            self.title,
            self.subtitle,
            [Button(self, text="Add record", style="Primary.TButton", command=self.add_record)],
        )
        # summary stat row
        self.stat_row = ttk.Frame(self, style="Page.TFrame")
        self.stat_row.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 18))
        self.stats = [
            Stat(self.stat_row, "Total records", tone="accent", glyph="✓"),
            Stat(self.stat_row, "This month", tone="info", glyph="◷"),
            Stat(self.stat_row, "Upcoming (30d)", tone="warn", glyph="⚑"),
            Stat(self.stat_row, "Total cost", tone="ok", glyph="₱"),
        ]
        for index, tile in enumerate(self.stats):
            self.stat_row.columnconfigure(index, weight=1, uniform="maint")
            tile.grid(row=0, column=index, sticky="nsew", padx=(0 if index == 0 else 16, 0))

        _wrap, pair = grid_row(
            self, [lambda master: Card(master), lambda master: Card(master)], weights=[3, 2], gap=18
        )
        _wrap.pack_configure(expand=True)
        records_card, upcoming_card = pair
        card_title(records_card, "Service history")
        self.table = Table(
            records_card.body,
            [
                ("vehicle", "Vehicle", 170, "w"),
                ("service_type", "Service", 110, "w"),
                ("performed_at", "Date", 96, "w"),
                ("odometer", "Odometer", 84, "e"),
                ("cost", "Cost", 80, "e"),
                ("next_due", "Next due", 96, "w"),
            ],
            on_double=lambda key: self.edit_record(int(key) if key else None),
        )
        self.table.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        self.empty_state = ttk.Frame(records_card.body, style="Card.TFrame")

        card_title(upcoming_card, "Upcoming reminders")
        self.upcoming_list = ttk.Frame(upcoming_card.body, style="Card.TFrame")
        self.upcoming_list.pack(fill="x", pady=(14, 0))
        self.refresh()

    def add_record(self) -> None:
        safe_dialog(
            self,
            lambda: MaintenanceDialog(self, self.store, actor=self.app.user["username"]),
            on_success=lambda: (self.refresh(), self.app.refresh_all()),
        )

    def edit_record(self, record_id: int | None) -> None:
        if record_id is None:
            return
        record = self.store.row("SELECT * FROM maintenance_records WHERE id = ?", (record_id,))
        if record is None:
            return
        safe_dialog(
            self,
            lambda: MaintenanceDialog(
                self, self.store, record=record, actor=self.app.user["username"]
            ),
            on_success=lambda: (self.refresh(), self.app.refresh_all()),
        )

    def delete_selected(self) -> None:
        key = self.table.selected()
        if not key:
            messagebox.showinfo(APP_NAME, "Select a record to delete first.", parent=self)
            return
        if not confirm_delete(self, "this maintenance record"):
            return
        store_action(
            self,
            lambda: self.store.delete_maintenance(int(key), actor=self.app.user["username"]),
            on_success=lambda: (self.refresh(), self.app.refresh_all()),
        )

    def refresh(self) -> None:
        summary = self.store.maintenance_summary()
        self.stats[0].set(str(summary["total"]), "All-time records")
        self.stats[1].set(str(summary["this_month"]), "Serviced this month")
        self.stats[2].set(str(summary["upcoming_30d"]), "Due in next 30 days")
        self.stats[3].set(money(summary["total_cost"]), "All-time spend")
        records = self.store.maintenance_records()
        # toggle the empty-state placeholder so the card never looks unfinished
        for child in getattr(self, "empty_state", ttk.Frame(self)).winfo_children():
            child.destroy()
        if not records:
            self.table.pack_forget()
            self.empty_state.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
            empty_state_block(
                self.empty_state,
                "No service records yet",
                "Log the first service visit to start the history.",
            )
        else:
            self.empty_state.pack_forget()
            self.table.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        prepared = []
        for row in records:
            prepared.append(
                {
                    "id": row["id"],
                    "vehicle": f"{row['plate_number']} {row['brand']} {row['model']}",
                    "service_type": row["service_type"].replace("_", " ").title(),
                    "performed_at": short_date(row["performed_at"]),
                    "odometer": f"{row['odometer']:,}" if row["odometer"] else "—",
                    "cost": money(row["cost"]),
                    "next_due": short_date(row["next_due_date"]) if row["next_due_date"] else "—",
                }
            )
        self.table.set_rows(prepared)
        for child in self.upcoming_list.winfo_children():
            child.destroy()
        upcoming = self.store.upcoming_maintenance(30)
        expiring_docs = self.store.expiring_documents(30)
        if not upcoming and not expiring_docs:
            empty_state_block(
                self.upcoming_list,
                "Nothing due",
                "Service reminders and expiring documents will appear here.",
            )
        for item in upcoming[:6]:
            row = ttk.Frame(self.upcoming_list, style="Card.TFrame")
            row.pack(fill="x", pady=4)
            swatch = tk.Canvas(
                row, width=4, height=24, background=PALETTE["surface"], highlightthickness=0, bd=0
            )
            swatch.create_rectangle(0, 0, 4, 24, fill=PALETTE["warn"], outline="")
            swatch.pack(side="left", padx=(0, 10))
            text = ttk.Frame(row, style="Card.TFrame")
            text.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)
            ttk.Label(
                text,
                text=f"{item['plate_number']} {item['brand']} {item['model']}",
                style="BodyCard.TLabel",
            ).pack(anchor="w")
            ttk.Label(
                text,
                text=f"{item['service_type'].replace('_', ' ')} due {item['next_due_date']}",
                style="MutedCard.TLabel",
            ).pack(anchor="w")
        for doc in expiring_docs[:4]:
            row = ttk.Frame(self.upcoming_list, style="Card.TFrame")
            row.pack(fill="x", pady=4)
            swatch = tk.Canvas(
                row, width=4, height=24, background=PALETTE["surface"], highlightthickness=0, bd=0
            )
            swatch.create_rectangle(0, 0, 4, 24, fill=PALETTE["danger"], outline="")
            swatch.pack(side="left", padx=(0, 10))
            text = ttk.Frame(row, style="Card.TFrame")
            text.pack(side="left", fill="x", expand=True)
            ttk.Label(
                text,
                text=f"{doc['plate_number']} {doc['brand']} {doc['model']}",
                style="BodyCard.TLabel",
            ).pack(anchor="w")
            ttk.Label(
                text,
                text=f"{doc['doc_type'].title()} expires {doc['expiry_date']}",
                style="MutedCard.TLabel",
            ).pack(anchor="w")


class ReportsPage(Page):
    key = "reports"
    title = "Reports"
    subtitle = "Export any ledger to CSV"

    def build(self) -> None:
        heading(self, self.title, self.subtitle)
        # The eleven report cards stack four rows tall — on a short window or
        # under high-DPI fonts they overflow, so the whole page scrolls
        # (same pattern as Flood zones and Alerts) instead of clipping the
        # last row's Export button.
        scroller = ScrollColumn(self, height=380, surface=False)
        scroller.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        host = scroller.inner
        grid = ttk.Frame(host, style="Page.TFrame")
        grid.pack(fill="x", pady=(0, 12))
        for index, key in enumerate(self.store.REPORTS):
            title, description = self.store.REPORTS[key]
            columns, rows = self.store.report(key)
            card = Card(grid, padding=(18, 16))
            card.grid(
                row=index // 3,
                column=index % 3,
                sticky="nsew",
                padx=(0 if index % 3 == 0 else 14),
                pady=(0 if index < 3 else 14),
            )
            ttk.Label(card.body, text=title, style="TitleCard.TLabel").pack(anchor="w")
            ttk.Label(card.body, text=f"{len(rows)} rows", style="MicroCard.TLabel").pack(
                anchor="w", pady=(2, 0)
            )
            ttk.Label(
                card.body,
                text=description,
                style="MutedCard.TLabel",
                wraplength=round(220 * ui_scale()),
                justify="left",
            ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(8, 0))
            Button(
                card.body,
                text="Export CSV",
                style="Secondary.TButton",
                command=lambda k=key: export_csv(self, k, *self.store.report(k)),
            ).pack(anchor="w", pady=(12, 0))
        for column in range(3):
            grid.columnconfigure(column, weight=1, uniform="reports")
        preview = Card(host)
        preview.pack(fill="x", pady=(2, 12))
        card_title(preview, "Preview")
        self.table = Table(
            preview.body,
            [("0", "Column 1", 160, "w"), ("1", "Column 2", 200, "w"), ("2", "Column 3", 200, "w")],
            height=9,
        )
        self.table.pack(fill="both", expand=True)
        self.refresh()

    def refresh(self) -> None:
        columns, rows = self.store.report("bookings")
        keys = [f"col{index}" for index in range(len(columns))]
        self.table.tree.configure(columns=keys)
        for index, name in enumerate(columns):
            self.table.tree.heading(keys[index], text=name)
            self.table.tree.column(keys[index], width=120, anchor="w")
        self.table.keys = keys
        self.table.set_rows(
            [
                {
                    **{key: "" for key in keys},
                    **{keys[index]: str(value) for index, value in enumerate(row)},
                }
                for row in rows[:8]
            ]
        )


def format_path_for_label(value: str, chars_per_line: int = 34) -> str:
    """Break a file-system path into lines at separator boundaries.

    A raw path is one unbroken token — a wrapping label would split it
    mid-word ("…app_dat / a"), which reads as corrupted. Breaking after
    ``/`` or ``\\`` keeps every segment intact and still fills each line.
    """
    if len(value) <= chars_per_line:
        return value
    lines: list[str] = []
    rest = value
    while len(rest) > chars_per_line:
        window = rest[: chars_per_line + 1]
        cut = max(window.rfind("/"), window.rfind("\\"))
        if cut < max(12, chars_per_line // 2):
            cut = chars_per_line
        lines.append(rest[:cut])
        rest = rest[cut:]
    lines.append(rest)
    return "\n".join(lines)


class SettingsPage(Page):
    key = "settings"
    title = "Settings"
    subtitle = "Organisation details, defaults and the local data folder used by the app"

    FIELDS = (
        ("organisation", "Organisation", None),
        ("contact_number", "Contact number", None),
        ("currency", "Currency", ["PHP", "USD", "SGD", "JPY"]),
        ("date_format", "Date format", [DATE_FORMAT, "%d/%m/%Y", "%m/%d/%Y"]),
        ("alert_threshold", "Minimum severity to alert", ALERT_SEVERITIES),
        ("zone_buffer_km", "Zone buffer", ["0.0", "0.5", "1.0", "2.0"]),
        ("map_detail", "Map detail", ["low", "medium", "high"]),
        ("map_zoom", "Map zoom", ["10", "11", "12", "13", "14"]),
    )
    SWITCHES = (
        ("sound_alerts", "Play a sound for critical alerts"),
        ("auto_scan", "Run the flood scan automatically every 15 minutes"),
        ("confirm_exit", "Ask before closing the application"),
    )

    def build(self) -> None:
        self.values: dict[str, tk.Variable] = {}
        self.status = tk.StringVar(value="")
        heading(
            self,
            self.title,
            self.subtitle,
            [
                Button(self, text="Backup now", style="Secondary.TButton", command=self.backup),
                Button(self, text="Save changes", style="Primary.TButton", command=self.save),
            ],
        )
        _wrap, pair = grid_row(
            self,
            [lambda master: Card(master), lambda master: Card(master)],
            weights=[13, 10],
            gap=14,
        )
        form_card, side_card = pair

        card_title(form_card, "Organisation")
        form = ttk.Frame(form_card.body, style="Card.TFrame")
        form.pack(fill="x", pady=(14, 0))
        settings = self.store.settings()
        for index, (key, label, choices) in enumerate(self.FIELDS):
            widget = Field(form, label, settings.get(key, ""), choices=choices, width=24)
            widget.grid(row=index // 2, column=index % 2, sticky="nsew", padx=(0, 14), pady=(0, 10))
            self.values[key] = widget.variable
        form.columnconfigure(0, weight=1)
        form.columnconfigure(1, weight=1)

        ttk.Label(form_card.body, text="BEHAVIOUR", style="MicroCard.TLabel").pack(
            anchor="w", pady=(12, 4)
        )
        for key, label in self.SWITCHES:
            variable = tk.BooleanVar(value=settings.get(key, "1") == "1")
            self.values[key] = variable
            ttk.Checkbutton(
                form_card.body, text=label, variable=variable, style="Switch.TCheckbutton"
            ).pack(anchor="w", pady=3)
        ttk.Label(form_card.body, textvariable=self.status, style="AccentCard.TLabel").pack(
            anchor="w", pady=(10, 0)
        )

        card_title(side_card, "Local storage")
        paths = ttk.Frame(side_card.body, style="Card.TFrame")
        paths.pack(fill="x", pady=(14, 0))
        for label, value in (
            ("Data folder", str(DATA_ROOT)),
            ("Database", str(DATABASE_FILE)),
            ("Exports", str(EXPORT_DIR)),
            ("Log file", str(LOG_FILE)),
        ):
            row = ttk.Frame(paths, style="Sunken.TFrame", padding=(11, 9))
            row.pack(fill="x", pady=4)
            ttk.Label(row, text=label.upper(), style="MicroSunken.TLabel").pack(anchor="w")
            ttk.Label(
                row,
                # break at separators — raw paths would wrap mid-word
                text=format_path_for_label(value),
                style="BodySunken.TLabel",
                justify="left",
            ).pack(anchor="w")

        about = Card(side_card)
        about.pack(fill="x", pady=(12, 0))
        card_title(about, "About")
        for label, value in (
            ("Application", APP_NAME),
            ("Version", APP_VERSION),
            ("Interface", "Tkinter / ttk"),
            ("Database", "SQLite 3 (single local file)"),
            ("Map data", "OpenStreetMap contributors"),
            ("Python", f"{sys.version.split()[0]}"),
        ):
            row = ttk.Frame(about.body, style="Card.TFrame")
            row.pack(fill="x", pady=2)
            ttk.Label(row, text=label, style="MutedCard.TLabel").pack(side="left")
            ttk.Label(row, text=value, style="BodyCard.TLabel").pack(side="right")
        self.refresh()

    def refresh(self) -> None:
        settings = self.store.settings()
        for key, variable in self.values.items():
            if isinstance(variable, tk.BooleanVar):
                variable.set(settings.get(key, "1") == "1")
            else:
                variable.set(settings.get(key, ""))

    def save(self) -> None:
        for key, variable in self.values.items():
            if isinstance(variable, tk.BooleanVar):
                value = "1" if variable.get() else "0"
            else:
                value = str(variable.get()).strip()
            self.store.set_setting(key, value)
        self.status.set("Settings saved.")
        self.app.refresh_all()
        self.after(2600, lambda: self.status.set(""))

    def backup(self) -> None:
        """Same flow as the File menu — one implementation, two entry points."""
        self.app._backup()
